[\[1b.1\]{D2628}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_sarba durga ti pa ri sho d+ha ya te dzo rA dzA ya ta thA ga tA ya ar+ha te samyak+saM bud+d+hA ya kalpa nA ma TI ka_/bod skad du/_de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par 
[\[1b.2\]]rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas ngan song thams cad yongs su sbyong ba'i gzi brjid kyi rgyal po zhes bya ba'i brtag pa'i bshad pa/_dpal rdo rje sems dpa' la phyag 'tshal lo/_/nyes pa'i skyon bral yon tan 
[\[1b.3\]]mtha' dag rdzogs/_/bde legs ji snyed thams cad 'byung ba'i gnas/_/sku gsum mchog gi bdag nyid rnam snang mdzad/_/gnas gsum 'dren pa'i gtso la phyag 'tshal lo/_/sku gsum gtsug tor 
[\[1b.4\]]bdag nyid rgyud chen las/_/rdo rje rtse mo'i kalpa'i rgyud 'di nyid/_/rab tu zab cing rgya che rtogs dka' ba'i/_/mdo yi don 'di mdo tsam dgrol bar bya/_/de la 'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na zhes bya ba la 
[\[1b.5\]]sogs pa 'di'i brjod par bya ba dang 'brel pa ni gang zhig yin/_de bzhin du dgos pa dang /_dgos pa'i dgos pa ni ci zhig yin zhe na/_de brjod par bya ste/_bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra 
[\[2a\]]
[\[2a.1\]]bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas gzi brjid kyi rgyal po'i brtag pa ngan song thams cad yongs su sbyong ba sku gsum gyi gtsug tor gyi rgyud chen po zhes bya ba 'di ni rgyun yin la/_'di'i 'di skad 
[\[2a.2\]]bdag gis zhes bya ba la sogs pa nas/_kun rig rnam par snang mdzad sku gsum gyi rgyud kyi bdag dang gzhan gyi don phun sum tshogs pa ston par byed pa yin te/_de yang dga' nas rjes su yi rang ngo bas gdul bya rnams 
[\[2a.3\]]'dod pa bskyed par bya ba'i phyir gsungs pa yin no/_/de la yang rgyud kyi don dbang po rab mgo smos pas go ba dang /_dbang po 'bring tshig dang don rnam par phye bas go ba rnams la ni rgyud kyi mtshan bcom ldan 'das de bzhin 
[\[2a.4\]]gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas ngan song thams cad yongs su sbyong ba gzi brjid kyi rgyal po'i brtag pa sku gsum gyi gtsug tor gyi rgyud chen po zhes bya bas kyang dbu zhabs kyi don rtogs par 'gyur la/_dbang 
[\[2a.5\]]po 'bring tshig dang don rnam par phye bas go ba rnams la ni de nyid kyi don rnam par phye bas go ba yin te/_de nyid kyi don yang bcom ldan 'das zhes bya ba la sogs pa ste/_bcom pa ni nyon mongs pa'i sgrib pa dang /_shes bya'i sgrib pa gnyis 
[\[2b\]]
[\[2b.1\]]gtan spangs pas phung po'i bdud la sogs pa bzhi bcom pa'o/_/ldan pa ni legs pa'i yon tan drug dang ldan pas na bcom ldan 'das te/_de yang /_nyon mongs shes rab kyis thub ste/_/de'i phyir shes rab 'joms zhes bya/_/dbang phyug la sogs drug ldan pas/_/bcom ldan 'das zhes 
[\[2b.2\]]rab tu brjod/_/ces so/_/mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa lnga rdzogs shing chos thams cad ngo bo nyid med pa stong pa nyid kyi chos nyid du sangs rgyas pa la ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas zhes bya'o/_/de yang /_gsang ba chen po tshogs mtho ba/_/zhes bya 
[\[2b.3\]]ba'i rgyud las/_snga ma ji ltar gshegs pa bzhin/_/mngon par byang chub rnam lnga yis/_/chos rnams thams cad dngos med par/_/rdzogs sangs rgyas las sangs rgyas brjod/_/ces gsungs so/_/de yang sku gsum gyi bdag nyid de bdag gi don phun sum tshogs pa'i bdag nyid 
[\[2b.4\]]du bstan to/_/de la gnyen po phun sum tshogs pa ni gzi brjid kyi rgyal po zhes bya ste/_bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad dam/_shAkya thub pa'i thugs ka nas nang sems kyi dkyil 'khor chen po'i 'od zer gyis ngan song thams cad sbyangs pas na gzi brjid kyi rgyal po zhes bya'o/_/brtag 
[\[2b.5\]]pa zhes bya ba ni thabs kyi dkyil 'khor phun sum tshogs pa 'gro ba'i don du sna tshogs pa'i dkyil 'khor du snang bar brtag pa'o/_/de'i phyir yang gsungs pa/_rnam par mi rtog chos dag las/_/sems can don du yongs su rtog_/rtog pas brtag pa las byung ba/_/rdo rje sems dpa' 
[\[2b.6\]]sems dpa' che/_/zhes gsungs te/_de bas na rdo rje sems dpa' nyid de rtog pa'i lha rnams kun kyang sarba bita kyi lha rnams su gyur nas mos pa ji ltar 'dod pa bzhin du sems can gyi don gyi dbang gis snang ngo /_/de'i phyir na de bzhin gshegs pa dang rigs kyi lha rnams thams 
[\[2b.7\]]cad kyi dkyil 'khor bcu gnyis bsdus pa'i don du yongs su brtag pa de'o/_/gzhan gyi don phun sum tshogs pa bstan pa'i phyir ngan song thams cad yongs su sbyong ba zhes bya ste/_de yang rgyu sbyangs pa dang /_'bras bu sbyangs pa dang /_rkyen sbyangs pa gsum gyis 'bras bu ngan 
[\[3a\]]
[\[3a.1\]]song gsum sbyang ngo /_/thams cad ni khams gsum ka'o/_/yongs su sbyong ba ni nang sems kyi dkyil 'khor te phyag rgya dang /_snying po ste bcu gcig pa'o/_/sngags dang rig pa sbyong ba'i phyir na sbyong ba zhes bya'o/_/sbyong ba'i thabs ji lta bu zhig gis sbyong 
[\[3a.2\]]snyam pa la/_bcom ldan 'das tshangs pa chen po pad+ma'i gdan la bzhugs te/_ngan song gi rgyud thams cad yongs su sbyong ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/_ngan song gi rgyud las rnam par grol ba zhes bya ba'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen 
[\[3a.3\]]po'i 'od zer sdud pa'i phreng ba mang po nyid kyis dpral ba'i mdzod spu nas byung bas stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad khyab par byas nas zhes bya ba la sogs pa'o/_/de la sbyong ba rnam pa gsum gyi sgo nas mtshon par byed pa'i phyir na sku gsum gyi gtsug tor 
[\[3a.4\]]gyi rgyud chen po zhes bya ba gsungs te/_de la nyon mongs pa lnga'i 'bras bu mtshams med pa lnga byas pa dag mnar med pa'i dmyal bar ltung ba de las bskyab pa'i phyir rnam par snang mdzad sku'i rang bzhin longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku'i dkyil 'khor du rtsa ba'i rgyud grub par mdzad pa dang /_
[\[3a.5\]]de bzhin gshegs pa gsung gi rang bzhin sprul pa'i sku'i dkyil 'khor du de dag gi rkyen du gyur pa'i sgrib pa lnga po de dag rnam par sbyong ba'i phyi ma'i rgyud grub par mdzad pa dang /_de dag gi rgyur gyur pa'i nyon mongs pa lnga'i rang bzhin can sbyong ba'i phyir na de bzhin gshegs pa'i thugs kyi 
[\[3a.6\]]rang bzhin chos kyi sku'i dkyil 'khor phyi ma'i yang phyi ma'i rgyud du grub par mdzad pas na sku gsum gyi gtsug tor gyi rgyud chen po zhes bya'o/_/de la nyon mongs pa'i 'bras bu mnar med pa'i dmyal ba chen por ltung ba de las bskyab pa ni dang po'i dkyil 'khor gyi dgos pa'o/_/rgyu dang 
[\[3a.7\]]rkyen sbyangs nas 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i bde ba phun sum tshogs pa grub par mdzad pa ni sku gnyis nye bar mtshon pa'i sgo nas phyi ma dang phyi ma'i rgyud kyis byed de dgos pa'i yang dgos pa'o/_/de'i yang de'i don la snyegs pa ni 'di yin te/_ye shes shin tu rnam par 
[\[3b\]]
[\[3b.1\]]dag pa'i dbang gis nyon mongs pa dang shes bya'i sgrib pa thams cad ni spangs pa nam mkha' bzhin du spros pa'i bag chags kyi tha snyad thams cad ni bsal ba'i rang bzhin gyis gsal ba chos kyi rang bzhin yin no/_/de ltar yin mod kyang yongs su dag pa dang ma dag pa'i sems can gdul ba'i don du sngon gyi 
[\[3b.2\]]smon lam gyi shugs kyis sku'i bkod pa dang /_gsung gi bkod pa dang /_ye shes rnam par dag pa'i dkyil 'khor don dam pa dang /_kun rdzob kyi yul 'di grub par mdzad nas ngan song gi sdug bsngal rnams gnyen po ye shes dang /_thabs kyi dkyil 'khor phun sum tshogs pa'i sgo nas phan 
[\[3b.3\]]'dogs par mdzad pa la ni bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas gzi brjid kyi rgyal po'i brtag pa ngan song thams cad yongs su sbyong ba zhes bya ba sku gsum gyi btsug tor gyi rgyud chen po'o/_/de yan chad kyis ni rgyud kyi mtshan blo 'bring don rnam 
[\[3b.4\]]par phye bas go ba dang dgos 'brel bstan nas/_da ni dbang po tha ma tshig dang yi ge lhur blangs pas go ba'i dbang du byas nas bshad de/_de la mdo sde 'di la yang bsdus te bstan pa dang rgyas par bstan pa'o/_/bsdus par bstan pa la yang sku gsung thugs kyis bstan pa'i dang po la don rnam pa 
[\[3b.5\]]gsum ste/_dang por gleng gzhi bstan pa dang /_gleng bslang ba bstan pa dang /_gzhung rgyas par bstan pa'o/_/gleng gzhi la yang phun sum tshogs pa bzhi ste/_dus phun sum tshogs pa dang /_ston pa phun sum tshogs pa dang /_gnas phun sum tshogs pa dang /_'khor phun sum tshogs 
[\[3b.6\]]pa dang bzhir bstan to/_/de la ci'i phyir yang dag par sdud par byed pas thog mar gleng gzhi smos she na/_de ni rgyu gnyis kyi phyir smos te/_rgyu gnyis ni yang dag par sdud par byed pa bdag la yid ches par byed pa bskyed pa'i phyir yang thog mar gleng gzhi smos la/_yid brtan du rung bas 
[\[3b.7\]]sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs par bstan pas de la gus pa bskyed pa'i phyir yang dag par sdud par byed pas thog mar gleng gzhi smos so/_/gleng bslang ba la yang gnyis te/_ston pas gleng bslang ba dang /_'khor gyis gleng bslang ba'o/_/gzhung rgyas par bstan pa la yang bsdus pa'i

[\[4a.1\]]don bcu gnyis te/_de yang gsum du shes par bya ste/_rtsa ba'i rgyud dang /_phyi ma'i rgyud dang /_phyi ma'i yang phyi ma'i rgyud do/_/de la 'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na zhes bya ba nas/_tshangs pa chen po pad+ma'i gdan la bzhugs te zhes bya ba yan chad kyis gleng 
[\[4a.2\]]gzhi bstan te/_de la yang bsgrub par bya ba dang /_sgrub par byed pa gnyis kyis bstan/_bsgrub par bya ba ni sdud pa pos bdag nyid tshad mar bya ba ste/_de yang yul dang dus dang rang gi ngo bo'i bye brag gis rnam pa gsum du bstan te/_de ni ma nor bar sdud pa po phun sum tshogs pa'o/_/ngan song gi rgyud 
[\[4a.3\]]thams cad yongs su sbyong ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te zhes bya ba nas gang de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis gdul bar mi 'gyur ba ni gnas ma yin no zhes bya ba yan chad kyis ni ston pas gleng bslang ba ste/_de la yang ston pa'i che ba nyid bstan pa'i zhar la gzhan don phun 
[\[4a.4\]]sum tshogs pa dang /_de la ngo mtshar skyes pa ngo mtshar gyi rgyu bcu the tshom bsal ba dang gsum gyis bstan to/_/de nas lha rnams kyi dbang po brgya byin gyis rang gi stan las langs te zhes bya ba nas bla na ma mchis pa'i yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub thob par bgyi ba'i slad du bshad par gsol zhes bya ba 
[\[4a.5\]]yan chad 'khor gyis gleng bslang bstan to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis brgya byin dang zhes bya ba nas/_dz+nyA na sa ma ye hUM zhes bya bas 'khor byin gyis brlabs pa bstan/_snyoms par zhugs nas yang zhes bya ba nas/_oM sha oM b+hA zhes bya ba yan chad kyis sbyor ba dang po dang /_
[\[4a.6\]]dkyil 'khor rgyal mchog dang /_nang sems kyi dkyil 'khor gyi ting nge 'dzin bstan/_lha'i dbang po de bzhin gshegs pa ngan song thams cad yongs su sbyong ba gzi brjid kyi rgyal po'i snying po 'di rigs kyi bu'am zhes bya ba nas bde ba thams cad myong bar 'gyur ro zhes bya ba yan chad kyis 
[\[4a.7\]]phan yon bstan/_de nas lha rnams kyi dbang po brgya byin gyis zhes bya ba nas/_de ni dkyil 'khor snying por gsungs/_/zhes bya ba yan chad kyis rang bzhin gyi dkyil 'khor bstan/_de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis sems can thams cad la thugs brtse ba'i slad du phyi'i dkyil 'khor 'di gsungs so zhes bya 
[\[4b\]]
[\[4b.1\]]ba nas de'i gtor ma ji ltar mthun par bya zhes bya ba yan chad kyis slob dpon gyi las kyi rim pa'i cho ga rgyas par bshad/_de nas sngags pa bla med byang chub kyi/_/bla med gnas su slob ma smin par bya/_/zhes bya ba nas/_dngos grub myur du 'grub par 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba yan chad kyis slob ma gzhug pa'i 
[\[4b.2\]]cho ga rgyas par bstan/_de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis lha'i dbang po brgya byin dang zhes bya ba nas/_bran bzhin las kun byed par 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba yan chad kyis las kyi sgrub pa bstan/_de nas lha rnams kyi dbang po brgya byin gyis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to zhes bya ba nas/_bde 
[\[4b.3\]]gshegs rgyal ba nyid kyis gsungs/_/zhes bya ba yan chad kyis shi ba la phan gdags pa'i ched du dbang bskur ba dang /_lha'i gzugs su brtags pa dang /_khyim du bzhag pa dang /_mchod rten bya ba dang /_sngags kyis phan gdags pa dang /_sbyin sreg gis phan gdags pa dang /_ro bsregs 
[\[4b.4\]]pas phan gdags pa dang /_mi 'grub pa'i skyon yon bstan pa'o/_/de nas brgya byin la sogs lha/_/zhes bya ba nas bsti stang du byas te phyag btsal nas de bzhin du song ngo zhes bya ba yan chad kyis/_dri ma med pa'i skye gnas dang /_cho 'phrul dang /_mchod pa bstan to/_/de nas brgya byin dang tshangs pa 
[\[4b.5\]]la sogs pa'i lha rnams ya mtshan du gyur te zhes bya ba nas bcom ldan 'das bdag cag gis gdon mi za bar sangs rgyas nyid du 'grub pa'i snod du gyur pa la sogs so zhes bya ba yan chad kyis rgyud kyi yon tan bstan/_bcom ldan 'das brtag pa'i rgyal po 'dis sems can gyi don bgyid 
[\[4b.6\]]pa gang lags pa'i skyes bu dam pa zhes bya ba nas bcom ldan 'das bdag cag gis zhu ba bzhin du bskyang bar bgyi'o zhes bya ba yan chad kyis 'chang ba'i skyes bu ji ltar blta ba dang /_'khor gyi dam bca' ba bstan to/_/lha'i dbang po khyod kyis ji ltar smras pa de bzhin te zhes bya ba nas bag yod par byos la spyod 
[\[4b.7\]]cig ces pa yan chad kyis rtsa ba'i rgyud gtad pa bstan to/_/de yan chad kyis ni rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi don bsdus ba'i bshad pa rdzogs so//_//da ni 'di nas rgyud phyi ma'i don ston te/_de nas bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po phyag na rdo rjes zhes bya ba nas thub pa'i

[\[5a.1\]]gtso bo la phyag 'tshal nas zhes bya ba'i bar gyis ni rgyud kyi 'chad pa po'i mtshan nyid bstan to/_/de nas sgrib pa thams cad rnam par dag pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas zhes bya ba nas bi na sha ni hUM phaT ces bya ba yan chad kyi sbyor ba

[\[5a.3\]]thams cad byed par 'gyur ro zhes bya ba yan chad kyis slob ma 'jug pa'i rim pa dang de'i yon tan bstan to/_/lha dang klu dang zhes bya ba nas ngan song thams cad las grol bar byed do zhes bya ba yan chad kyis shi ba la phan gdags pa bstan to/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po phyag

[\[5a.5\]]ba nas lha'i 'khor 'di nyid yin no zhes bya ba yan chad kyi lha'i bu nor bu dri ma med pa de'i sngon gyi las [(]gyi [,]kyi [)]rnam par smin pa dang /_da ltar gar skyes pa'i lung bstan pa dang /_sngon dang da ltar gyi rgyu mthun gyi smon lam gyis thar pa'i tshul bstan to/_/da ni tshe dpag med kyi dkyil 'khor bstan te/_de nas 
[\[5a.6\]]brgya byin dang zhes bya ba nas legs par sgrubs shig bshad par bya'o zhes bya ba yan chad kyis ming bcu pas bstod pa dang /_gsol ba gdab pa dang /_gnang ba bstan to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rjes zhes bya ba nas rmad kyi ya mtshan du ma 'jig rten du 'byung ngo zhes bya ba yan chad kyis sbyor

[\[5b\]]
[\[5b.1\]]sbrul gyi 'jigs pa rnams/_/zhes bya ba nas de ltar dbang skur nas zhes bya ba yan chad kyis slob ma gzhug pa dang /_dbang bskur ba'i cho ga rgyas pa'o/_/tshe bsring ba'i rig pa 'di sbyin zhes bya ba nas the tshom med do zhes bya ba yan chad kyis ras kyi sgrub pa dang /_mtshan ma dang

[\[5b.4\]]'khor rgyal po mchog gi ting nge 'dzin bstan to/_/de nas de dag gis dkyil 'khor bshad de zhes bya ba nas sgo ba rnams kyang de bzhin no zhes bya ba yan chad kyis dkyil 'khor gyi lha bstan la/_de nas phyag rgya dang ldan pas zhes bya ba nas/_cho ga bzhin du mchod pa dbul/_/zhes bya ba yan chad kyis slob dpon

[\[10b.2\]]ge a'i sgo nas sgrib pa bcom pa dang /_chos kyi sku stong pa nyid dang ldan pas bcom ldan 'das so/_/yon tan gyi khyad par de lta bu dang ldan pa'i bcom ldan 'das de gnas gang na bzhugs snyam pa la/_lha'i mchog ces pa ste/_lha thams cad kyi mchog tu gyur pa lha'i dbang po brgya 
[\[10b.3\]]byin te/_'di shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i don la bskal pa dpag tu med par sbyangs pas na lha'i mchog go/_/lha dang mi la sogs pa thams cad kyi yang yid kyi dbang po rtas par byed pas na thams cad kyi dga' ba'i tshal yid du 'ong ba'o/_/lha de dag gis longs spyod kyi 
[\[10b.4\]]rkyen tshal chen po bzhi yang yod de/_byang phyogs dga' ba'i tshal dang /_shar phyogs shing rta sna tshogs dang /_lho phyogs rtsub 'gyur dang /_nub phyogs 'dres pa'i tshal dang /_gzhan yang sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyis byin gyis brlabs pa na zhes bya 
[\[10b.5\]]ba dang sbyar te/_dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas thams cad kyis sngar byin gyis brlabs pa byas pa'o/_/de bas na nor bu dang zhes bya ba la sogs pa smos te/_nor bu ni ston pa'i che ba'i yon tan las byung ba ste/_gser gyi shing yal ga dang lcug mar bcas pa zhes bya ba ni dran pa nye bar gzhag 
[\[10b.6\]]pa bzhi dang /_yang dag par spong ba bzhi dang /_rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi rnam par dag pa las byung ba'o/_/shing gel pa ni dbang po lnga rnam par dag pa las byung ba'o/_/sman ni stobs lnga rnam par dag pa'o/_/me tog ka ma la la sogs pa bdun ni rim gyis byang chub kyi yan lag bdun 
[\[10b.7\]]rnam par dag pa las byung ba'o/_/dpag bsam gyi shing ni rnam par thar pa brgyad rnam par dag pa las byung ba'o/_/rgyan sna tshogs kyis brgyan pa ni dpa' bar 'gro ba'i ting nge 'dzin la sogs pa'i ting nge 'dzin 'bum phrag rnam par dag pa'o/_/bya rnam pa sna tshogs skad 'byin pa ni tshangs pa'i 
[\[11a\]]
[\[11a.1\]]gsung dbyangs kyi gsung rab yan lag bcu gnyis kyi sgra sgrogs pa'o/_/rnga dang cang te'u la sogs pa'i sgra grag pa ni/_pha rol tu phyin pa brgyad kyi mchod pa sgeg mo la sogs pa rnam par dag pa'o/_/brgya byin dang tshangs pa la sogs pa ni thabs dang shes rab 
[\[11a.2\]]kyi 'bras bu las/_thabs sbyin pa la sogs pa rnam par smin pa'i 'bras bu'o/_/rtsen pa zhes bya ba ni bstod pa'o/_/de lta bu gnas su byin gyis brlabs pas na sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyis byin gyis brlabs pa'o/_/na zhes bya bas ni bzhugs pa dang 
[\[11a.3\]]'brel to/_/de 'dra ba'i gnas de na 'khod pa'i 'khor phun sum tshogs pa ni gang dang gang zhe na/_brgya byin dang zhes bya ba la sogs pa smos te/_brgya byin ni sa dang po'i bden pa mthong bas skad cig ma cig la zhing khams brgya dang /_ting nge 'dzin brgya la dbang byed pas na brgya byin 
[\[11a.4\]]no/_/sa dang po 'khor ba gtan nas spangs nas/_'jig rten pa'i bdag po'i tshul gzung bas tshangs pa'o/_/rig 'dzin dang zhes pas ni so so'i skye bo las bla na med pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed de tshogs rnam pa gnyis bsags nas sbyor ba'i lam non/_der gzung 'dzin rags pa 
[\[11a.5\]]spangs nas mthong ba'i lam non te/_mi rtog pa'i sa la gnas pas rig 'dzin no/_/gnod sbyin ni rnam thos sras dang /_sa srung la sogs pa'o/_/srin po ni bden bral dang /_bum sna la sogs pa'o/_/dri za ni yul 'khor srung dang /_zur phud lnga pa la sogs pa'o/_/lha ma yin ni rab sim 
[\[11a.6\]]dang /_snang byed la sogs pa'o/_/lto 'phye ni rab brtan la sogs pa'o/_/mi'am ci ni ljon pa la sogs pa'o/_/klu ni dga' bo la sogs pa'o/_/du ma ni so so nas mang pos bskor ba'o/_/de ltar 'jig rten pa'i 'khor la smos pa ni de dag snod du rung bar bya dgos pa dang /_
[\[11a.7\]]phyis 'byung ba gzhan dag kyang dad pa bskyed pa dgos pa'i bya ba'i phyir ro/_/de la 'jig rten las 'das pa'i 'khor phun sum tshogs pa gang zhe na/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po bye ba khrag khrig phrag brgyad la 'di lta ste/_zhes pa ste de las byang chub sems 
[\[11b\]]
[\[11b.1\]]dpa' zhes bya ba ni bdag gi don phun sum tshogs pa la/_/sems dpa' chen po ni gzhan gyi don phun sum tshogs pa ste/_de la byang chub ni zad pa mi skye ba'o/_/sems dpa' ni thob par 'dod pa'o/_/sems dpa' chen po ni sems can gyi don bya ba la mi 'jigs shing mi 
[\[11b.2\]]skyo ba med par dam bca' zhing gnas pa ste/_don du na bdag gis bla na med pa'i byang chub tu byas la sems can thams cad 'khor ba'i btson ra shin tu dam pa dag las gdon par bya'o zhes so/_/rnam pa gcig tu byang chub ni don dam pa 'od gsal ba la bya ste/_de yang /_byang chub nam mkha'i 
[\[11b.3\]]mtshan nyid de/_/nam mkha' la yang mtshan nyid med/_/ces so/_/byang chub sems dpa' dag gis mtshan 'dir blo gros zhes ming gi don gtsor smos she na/_de ni thabs thugs rje chen po dang /_shes rab ste so sor brtags pa'i dbang gis 'gro ba dag ngan song las 'dren pa'i sgor gyur pas na de la de skad ces 
[\[11b.4\]]bya'o/_/byang chub sems dpa' phyag na rdo rje la ni blo gros tshad med pa zhes bya'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di'i mthu stobs ni tshad med do zhes bya ba'i rgyud las gsungs so/_/yang na 'di de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi byang chub kyi sems dang ba'i bdag nyid can gyi rang bzhin yin pas dang por 
[\[11b.5\]]smos la sdud pa po 'khor gyi dang po yin pas kyang dang por smos so/_/spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug la ni blo gros mi g.yo ba zhes bya'o/_/ci'i phyir zhe na/_'jigs pa chen po brgyad kyis zos pa'i sems can thams cad spyan ras gzigs kyis so sor rtog pa mdzad pa dang /_gzigs 
[\[11b.6\]]shing skyob par mdzad pa la gzhan gyis thugs mi g.yo bar mdzad pa'i phyir ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' nam mkha'i snying po la ni blo gros yangs pa zhes bya ste/_/ci'i phyir zhe na/_bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs kyis nam mkha'i dbyings la mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes la longs spyod 
[\[11b.7\]]pa'i phyir na yangs pa zhes bya'o/_/rdo rje khu tshur la ni kun du blo gros zhes bya'o/_/ci'i phyir zhe na/_chos thams cad rang bzhin gyis 'od gsal ba'o zhes bya ba 'dis/_chos thams cad gcig tu byed pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku gsung thugs kyi sgo thams cad gnyis su dbyer mi phyed pas 
[\[12a\]]
[\[12a.1\]]chos nyid kyi khur gang la mnga' ba de'o/_/de yang /_gang zhig mi slu bden de nyid/_/rdo rje khu tshur zhes bshad do/_/zhes lung las gsungs pa yin no/_/'jam dpal la ni blo gros mtha' yas zhes bya ste/_ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di ni gnyis su med pa'i shes rab kyi pha rol 
[\[12a.2\]]tu phyin pa la gnas pa'i rgyu las nges par 'byung ba'i de kho na nyid kyi chos kyi dbyings la mtha' yas pa'i phyir ro/_/rdo rje las la ni blo gros ka ma la zhes bya'o/_/ci'i phyir zhe na/_bran gyi tshul du de 'phags pa thams cad la mchod pa'i rnam pa thams cad stobs pa dang /_
[\[12a.3\]]sems can thams cad la sbyin pa'i las byed pa la gtsor gyur pas na'o/_/nam mkha' mdzod la ni blo gros chen po zhes bya'o/_/ci'i phyir zhe na/_mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes kyi mdzod 'di la mnga' bas sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa las byung ba'i nam mkha' mdzod kyi ting nge 'dzin 
[\[12a.4\]]gyis 'gro ba yongs su tshim pas na'o/_/rdo rje legs pa ni blo gros lha rdzas zhes bya'o/_/ci'i phyir zhe na/_rdo rje thams cad bdag la 'jug pa'i las byed pas lha rdzas kyi dgyes pa sbrel bar byed do zhes pa'o/_/blo gros sna tshogs ni bdud kyi stobs thams cad rab tu 'joms pa'o/_/
[\[12a.5\]]ci'i phyir zhe na/_phung po dang nyon mongs pa dang /_'chi bdag dang /_lha'i bu'i bdud thams cad de/_de'i stobs ni nus pa'o/_/de rab tu 'joms shing zlog par mdzad pa'i ngang tshul gang la mnga' ba de'o/_/phyir mi ldog pa ni sa bcu la gnas pa'o/_/byang chub sems 
[\[12a.6\]]dpa' 'di rnams ni bsgrub pa'i yan lag tu gyur pa yin te/_lung las dam pa'i chos kyi grong khyer skyong bar byed pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams zhes gsungs pa yin no/_/de lta bu'i 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i dbus na bcom ldan 'das tshangs pa chen po pad+ma'i gdan 
[\[12a.7\]]la tshangs pa'i bzhugs thabs kyis bzhugs zhes bya'o/_/'di lta ste pad+ma chu las skyes kyang rang bzhin gyis dag cing chu dang 'dam gyi nyes pas ma gos pa bzhin du sems can gyi don bsgrub pa'i phyir 'khor ba na gnas kyang de'i nyes pas mi gos pa'o/_/tshangs pa zhes pas pad+ma bkra shis 
[\[12b\]]
[\[12b.1\]]pa'i gnas pas na/_sa phyogs gang du skyes pa'i sa phyogs de gtsang mar gyur pa ji lta ba bzhin du bcom ldan 'das tshangs pa chen po gang du bzhugs pa'i gnas de yang gtsang zhing bkra shis pa'o/_/de yang lung las/_yon tan yang dag rgyan gyi dam pa dag_/gang na gnas par 
[\[12b.2\]]gyur pa de dag tu/_/bkra shis bkra dang ldan pa gyur pa ste/_/'bab stegs de yin dka' thub tshal yin no/_/zhes gsungs so/_/de'i phyir na pad+ma de yang tshangs pa zhes gdags so/_/tshangs pa'i gdan de la bcom ldan 'das tshangs pa chen po de bzhugs so/_/ci'i phyir na bcom 
[\[12b.3\]]ldan 'das la tshangs pa chen po zhes bya zhe na/_ma rig pa la sogs pa'i nyon mongs pa'i dri ma thams cad dag par mdzad pas na/_tshangs pa zhes bya'o/_/de'i phyir yang lung las gsungs pa/_ma rig 'joms pa'i tshangs pa ste/_/'dod chags zhe sdang dri mthar mdzad/_/
[\[12b.4\]]ces bya'o/_/bzhugs zhes bya ba ni rnam pa gsum ste/_spyod lam gyis bzhugs pa dang /_chos 'chad cing bzhugs pa dang /_nang du yang dag 'jog gis bzhugs pa'o/_/spyod lam gyis bzhugs pa ni 'chag pa dang /_'greng ba dang /_'dug pa la sogs pa yis so/_/
[\[12b.5\]]chos 'chad cing bzhugs pa ni mnyam pa'i skyil mo krung gis bzhugs nas phyag g.yon pa bram ze smrang 'don pa lta bu la/_phyag g.yas pa skyabs sbyin mdzad pas bzhugs pa'o/_/nang du yang dag 'jog gis bzhugs pa la yang rnam pa gsum gyis bzhugs te/_tshad med tshangs pa'i 
[\[12b.6\]]bzhugs thabs dang /_bsam gtan lha'i bzhugs thabs dang /_rnam thar 'phags pa'i bzhugs thabs las/_'dir ni tshad med tshangs pa'i bzhugs thabs so/_/bzhugs pa zhes bya ba ni rab tu rnam par gnas so zhes bya'o/_/de yang gang gi tshe na lha'i bu nor bu dri ma med pa de shi ba'i 
[\[12b.7\]]dus byas pa de'i tshe'o/_/gang zhig bzhugs na ston pa phun sum tshogs pa de'o/_/gang na bzhugs na dga' ba'i tshal na'o/_/gang dang bzhugs na 'khor 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa dang bzhugs zhes bya'o/_/de yan chad kyis ni gleng gzhi bstan pa'o/_/de yan chad kyis thun 
[\[13a\]]
[\[13a.1\]]mong gi gleng gzhi bstan nas/_da ni rgyud kyi don 'byung ba khyad par gyi gleng gzhi bshad par bya ste/_de yang lha'i bu nor bu dri ma med pa'i 'od mtshams med pa'i dmyal bar ltung ba de las yongs su bskyab pa'i phyir bcom ldan 'das kyis ye shes dri ma med pa'i 'od zer gyis gleng 
[\[13a.2\]]bslang ba'o/_/de yang gang la snyoms par zhugs she na/_ngan song gi rgyud thams cad yongs su sbyong ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin te/_nang sems kyi dkyil 'khor ni 'og nas ston to/_/ting nge 'dzin de'i ming gang zhe na/_ngan song gi rgyud las rnam par grol ba zhes bya'o/_/gnas gang nas 
[\[13a.3\]]byung zhe na/_nang sems kyi dkyil 'khor gyi thugs ka la nyid kyi dpral ba'i mdzod spu nas zhes bya'o/_/las gang du byed ce na/_'od zer des stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad snang bar byas so zhes so/_/ji ltar don byas na/_nang sems kyi dkyil 'khor 
[\[13a.4\]]du bcug nas dri ma med pa la sogs pa bde ba so sor thob par byas te/_drin la lan gyis blan te bstod pas bstod de/_de yang ji ltar bstod snyam pa la/_e ma sangs rgyas e sangs rgyas/_/zhes bya ba la sogs pa ste/_e ma ni ngom zhing ngo mtshar ba ste/_gang la ngom zhing 
[\[13a.5\]]ngo mtshar na/_nang sems kyi dkyil 'khor gyi 'od zer dang /_sngags kyi phreng ba'i 'od zer gyis sems can gyi las dang nyon mongs pa'i bag chags dag par byas/_nang sems kyi dkyil 'khor du bcug cing dbang bskur bas dri ma med par grags pa la sogs pa dga' ldan gyi lha'i 
[\[13a.6\]]lus thob par byas pas ngom zhing ngo mtshar ba'o/_/sangs rgyas ni bod pa'i tshig go/_/kye sangs rgyas 'gro ba'i don sna tshogs par mdzad pa ni re bzang zhes bya ba'o/_/kye ma sangs rgyas ni 'gro ba'i don ji ltar mdzad kyang thugs chos nyid las ma g.yos pas re bzang 
[\[13a.7\]]zhes bya'o/_/mdzad pa legs pa ni sku gsung thugs kyi phrin las sku gser btso ma lta bu ston pa'i cha byad dang /_gsang sngags tshig mang po dang /_thugs nang sems kyi dkyil 'khor gyis 'gro ba'i don mdzad pa'o/_/gang phyir ngan song bdag cag sbyangs/_/zhes 
[\[13b\]]
[\[13b.1\]]bya ba ni thabs dang shes rab zung du 'brel ba'i thugs rjes 'khor ba'i rgyu 'bras rkyen gsum sbyangs pa'o/_/byang chub spyod pa nyid la bzhag_/ces bya ba ni sems can de rnams 'khor ba las bton nas byang chub tu sems bskyed de dga' ldan gyi gnas su skye bar 
[\[13b.2\]]bya ba'i phyir na bstod pa des bstod pa'o/_/da ni bcom ldan 'das kyis de ltar mdzad pa la ngo mtshar bskyed pa de la ngo mtshar mi che ba'i rgyu ston te/_de yang lha'i dbang po brgya byin 'di ni ngo mtshar mi che ste zhes bya ba la sogs pa smos te/_de yang dang po sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'i sgo nas bstan pa ni tshad med 
[\[13b.3\]]pa'i bsod nams kyi tshogs zhes bya ba la sogs pa ste/_de la tshad med ces bya bas ni rgya che ba'i phyir dang /_gting dpag dka' ba'i phyir te/_de yang sems can bsod nams kyi phung po bcu 'gyur gyis ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i ba spu'i bu ga gcig 'grub po zhes bya ba nas/_gtsug tor 
[\[13b.4\]]du gtogs pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po bye ba 'bum gyis ni chos kyi dung 'grub bo zhes bya ba'i bar du ste/_de ni 'bras bu'i sgo nas so/_/rgyu ni sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dang byang chub kyi phyogs la ni bsod nams kyi tshogs zhes bya'o/_/'jig rten las 'das pa'i yon tan rin po che thams cad 
[\[13b.5\]]'byung ba'i phyir na bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa rnam par dag pa'o/_/thabs tshad med pa ni ji lta ji ltar 'dul ba'i thabs de la rgyu 'bras gnyis kyis ston te/_rgyu chung ngu las 'bras bu mang du ston la/_'bras bu'i thabs ni sems can thams cad ji ltar mos pa bzhin du ston pas na thabs kyi pha rol 
[\[13b.6\]]tu phyin pa rdzogs pa'o/_/gnyis su med pa'i shes rab kyi spyi dang rang gi mtshan nyid 'byed pa'i phyir na shes rab tshad med pa'o/_/spyod pa tshad med pa ni sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa ni tshul khrims gsum ste/_de la gnas pa ni tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o/_/bsam 
[\[13b.7\]]gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa las byung ba'i phyir byams pa dang /_snying rje dang dga' ba dang /_btang snyoms bzhis 'dul ba'i skye bo tshad med pa rnams snod du gyur par mdzad pa'o/_/thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyis brgyan pa'i phyir/_theg pa gzhan dang mi mnyam zhing theg pa chen po dang 
[\[14a\]]
[\[14a.1\]]mnyam pa'i ye shes dang ldan pa'o/_/brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa las byung ba'i rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi dang ldan pa'i phyir lha chen po dag dang /_rang sangs rgyas dag dang mi mnyam zhing /_sa bcu'i byang chub sems dpa' dag dang mnyam pa'i rdzu 'phrul dang ldan pa'o/_/
[\[14a.2\]]lha chen po dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_stobs po che dang /_rang sangs rgyas dag dang mi mnyam zhing /_sa bcu pa'i byang chub sems dpa' de dag dang /_sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das dag dang mnyam pa'i zhing nyid kyis theg pa 'og ma dag smon par byed pa'i smon lam gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang ldan 
[\[14a.3\]]pa'i phyir ro/_/lha dang stobs po che dang /_rang sangs rgyas dag dang /_gnod sbyin dag gis stobs dang mi mnyam zhing sangs rgyas dang /_byang chub sems dpa' de dag dang mnyam pa'i stobs bcu dang ldan pa ste/_rgyu dang rkyen de'i phyir na lha'i dbang po ngo mtshar mi che'o zhes 
[\[14a.4\]]bya'o/_/'di la the tshom dang som nyi yid gnyis ma byed cig ces pa ni/_the tshom ni yid ma ches pas rtog pa zhugs pa'o/_/som nyi ni bya ba'am mi bya ba'am snyam pa'i som nyi'o/_/yid gnyis ni res blo gtod res blo mi gtod pa'o/_/de ni ston pas gleng bslang bas ngo 
[\[14a.5\]]mtshar gyi rgyu bstan pa'o/_/da ni 'khor gyis zhus pa ston te/_de nas lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyis rang gis stan las langs nas zhes bya ba la sogs pa la/_'dod khams kyi lha la dbang byed pa dang /_sbyin pa dang /_sprul pa dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang /_zhing khams brgya la 
[\[14a.6\]]dbang byed pas na brgya byin no/_/rang gi stan ni rin po che'i khri'o/_/chos rag lus pa dang /_rim gro bskyed pa dang /_gus pa bskyed pa'i phyir ro/_/mchod pa ni rdzas rgya che ba dang /_yid kyis sprul pa'i lha mo'i tshogs kyis mchod pa ni rgya che zhing che ba'o/_/phyag ni lus 
[\[14a.7\]]ngag yid gsum mo/_/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to zhes bya ba ni blo la gnas pa ste/_'og gi tshig go/_/phan pa zhes bya ba ni sems can thams cad la sems bskyed pa'o/_/snying rje ba ni phan 'dogs pa'o/_/snying rje can ni sdig pa chen po byas pa'o/_/snying brtse 
[\[14b\]]
[\[14b.1\]]ba chen po can ni ngan song du lhung ba'i don to/_/spobs pa bskyed pa zhes bya ba ni bsod nams kyi tshogs dang /_mos pa'i mthu dang /_rgyu dang 'bras bu zhu ba'i spobs pa bskyed du gsol zhes pa'o/_/bcom ldan 'das sum cu rtsa gsum gyi gnas 'di nyid nas zhes bya ba nas/_bshad 
[\[14b.2\]]par bya ba yan chad kyis gsol pa dang /_lung bstan pa dang /_'jigs shing skrag pa bstan nas gsol ba gdab pa bstan te dkyus nas gsal ba'o/_/de yan chad gleng gzhi dang /_gleng brjod 'byung ba khyad par gyi don bstan nas/_da ni gzhung bstan par bya ba las/_
[\[14b.3\]]dang por rtsa ba'i dkyil 'khor sbyor ba dang po dang /_dkyil 'khor rgyal po mchog dang /_nang sems kyi dkyil 'khor dang /_slob ma'am 'khor snod du rung bar bya ba bstan te/_de la dang por slob ma snod du rung bar bya ba'i phyir dang /_dam tshig can du byin gyis brlabs pa 
[\[14b.4\]]bstan to/_/de nas zhes bya ba ni skabs so/_/de yang gang gis byin gyis brlabs na bcom ldan 'das kyis so/_/gang la byin gyis brlabs na brgya byin dang tshangs pa la sogs pa'i lha'i bu de dag ste/_byin gyis brlabs pa'i ting nge 'dzin gang la snyoms par zhugs na gdon mi za ba'i rdo rje 
[\[14b.5\]]byin gyis brlabs pa'i ting nge 'dzin to/_/nyid kyi thugs ka nas 'od zer sbud pa'i phreng ba spros pas/_de na gnas pa'i sems can rnams rgyu las dang nyon mongs pa'i bcings pa thams cad bkrol zhing bcad/_'bras bu sdug bsngal gyis bcings pa thams cad ni bcom zhing sbyangs te/
[\[14b.6\]]slar 'dus pa las brgya byin dang tshangs pa la sogs pa 'jig rten pa'i 'khor rnams kyi nyon mongs pa thams cad sbyangs nas bka'i snod du rung ba'am/_dam tshig can du byin gyis brlabs so/_/de nas brgya byin dang tshangs pa la sogs pa'i snying gar zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gang zhing yongs su rdzogs pa'i 
[\[14b.7\]]steng du'o/_/sngags gang gis she na/_oM badzra a d+hi tiSh+Tha dz+nyA na sa ma ye hUM zhes pa'i sngags kyi phreng ba bkod nas dam tshig can du byin gyis brlabs so/_/de ni dkyil 'khor byin gyis brlabs pa'o/_/da ni sbyor ba dang po ting nge 'dzin ston te/_gdon mi za bar mi choms pa'i rdo rjes byin 
[\[15a\]]
[\[15a.1\]]gyis brlabs nas zhes bya ba smos te/_gdon mi za bar zhes bya ba ni rang gi sems so sor rig pa ste/_phyi'i yul du gyur pa'i snod kyi 'jig rten rdul phra rab 'dus pa las grub pa ni phyogs cha drug gi chas gzhigs na rdul phra rab tsam du yang ma grub pa stong pa chen po'i ngang 
[\[15a.2\]]du bsgoms la/_de la nang 'dzin pa'i sems la brtags na ni sems cir yang ma grub ste/_rang bzhin gyis 'od gsal ba'i yi ge a'i dngos por gnas par bsgom mo/_/de nas a de las sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yon tan rnams 'dir gzugs brnyan gyi rnam pa'i tshul gyis/_/
[\[15a.3\]]a A i I u U r-i r-I l-i l-I e ai o au aM aH/_rab tu zhugs te shel dang zla ba lta bu'o/_/de yang /_dag pa chen po'i snying pa dag_/nam mkha' bzhin du dri ma med/_/gnas ngan len pa kun las grol/_/rnam rtog sel ba'i mchog yin no/_/nyon mongs dri ma 
[\[15a.4\]]kun dag cing /_/zla ba bzhin du snga ma'i steng /_/zhes so/_/de la ni gdon mi za bar zhes brjod do/_/de nas rang gi gsang sngags zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas pa bkod nas sangs rgyas kyi yon tan thams cad skar ma'i rnam pa lta bus gzugs brnyan la sogs pa'i tshul 
[\[15a.5\]]gyis yi ge ka la sogs pa rab tu zhugs te zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gnyis par gyur te/_nyam thag rnams la rjes brtse bas/_/sems can yongs su 'dzin pas ni/_/dge ba'i chos rnams rdzogs pa ste/_/nyon mongs thams cad nges 'du'o/_/zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus 
[\[15a.6\]]su ni/_/byang chub sems ni gnyis pa'o/_/zhes gsungs so/_/de la ni mi choms pa zhes bya'o/_/de nas de lta bu'i zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi steng du dbus su hUM tha ma na tshur oM sho d+ha ne sho d+ha ne/_sarba pA paM bi sho d+ha ni/_shud+d+he bi shud+d+he sarba karma A ba ra Na bi shud+d+he swA hA/_zhes 
[\[15a.7\]]pa'i sngags kyi phreng ba de rnams kyang mar me'i phreng ba bskor ba ltar g.yo ba dang /_ldem pa dang 'od dang zer du bcas pa rnams dang /_rnam par shes pa mi gnas na yang gnas par gnas bzhin du bsgom mo/_/de las 'od zer spro ba dang /_bsdu bas rdo rje rtse lnga bar bsgom ste/_de yang gsungs 
[\[15b\]]
[\[15b.1\]]pa/_byang chub bskal pa grangs med gsum/_/bsdus pa dag ni chos nyid de/_/dam chos mchog gi grangs med pa'i/_/spyad pa spyod pa'i mchog yin no/_/sa dang sa min legs rtogs shing /_/zag med ye shes dag pa'o/_/zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus su ni/_/rdo rje chos 
[\[15b.2\]]can gyis brtags pa/_/zhes bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'chang chen pos gsungs so/_/de la ni rdo rje zhes bya'o/_/byin gyis brlabs nas zhes bya ba ni/_de las 'od zer spro ba dang bsdu ba'i sbyor bas ye shes lnga la sogs pa spros pa thams cad dang bral ba'i ye shes thams 
[\[15b.3\]]cad dang /_lha thams cad kyi sku rdo rje dbyings dang /_gsung dang thugs thams cad kyi rdo rje dbyings thams cad snying gar bsdus te gcig tu brtan par bya'o/_/de yang gsungs pa/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi sku yang yin/_/skyon med snang ba med pa ste/_/bcos min ma skyes 
[\[15b.4\]]dag pa dang /_/dngos med la sogs rnam spangs pa/_/rdzas med chos kyi sku nyid ni/_/'di ni mi chod mi shig pa'o/_/rdo rje bdag nyid legs 'byung ba'i/_/rdo rje sku ni bla med pa'o/_/zhes gsungs so/_/ngan song thams cad yongs su sbyong ba'i rgyal po zhes bya ba kun rig rnam par snang 
[\[15b.5\]]mdzad kyi skur phyag rgya chen por gyur te/_lus dang de bzhin sbyong ba dang /_/sbyong dang de bzhin rtogs pa dang /_/mngon par byang chub rnam pa lnga/_/sangs rgyas kun bdag bzang po'o/_/zhes so/_/sku mdog ni dung ltar dkar ba/_me tog kun da ltar snum la 'dril ba/_zla ba ltar 'od dang 
[\[15b.6\]]ldan pa zhal bzhi pa la dbu mthon mthing lcang lor 'khyil ba/_/rdo rje mtshan pa'i seng ge'i gdan pad+ma kha dog sna tshogs pa'i dbus su zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi steng na rdo rje skyil krung du bzhugs pa/_rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa/_ting nge 'dzin gyi phyag rgya dang ldan par bsgoms te de ltar 'di 
[\[15b.7\]]nyid las/_/dbus kyi phyogs su kun rig ni/_dung dang kun da zla 'dra la/_/zhal bzhi pa la rdo rje yis/_/seng ge'i gdan la bzhugs pa dang /_/ting 'dzin phyag rgya ldan pa dang /_/rgyan rnams kun gyis brgyan par bri/_/zhes pa'o/_/de nas nam mkha' las byung pa'i rin po che'i dbang 
[\[16a\]]
[\[16a.1\]]bskur ba dang /_spyan ras gzigs kyi chos kyi ye shes bskyed pa dang /_cho 'phrul bzhis kyang byin gyis brlabs te/_snying gar rdo rje bsgoms pa dang /_dpral bar rin po che bsgom pa dang /_khar pad+ma bsgom pa dang /_spyi bor rgya gram bsgom pa ni byin gyis brlabs pa 
[\[16a.2\]]dang /_dbang bskur ba dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang /_mchod pa'i cho 'phrul lo zhes slob dpon la la dag bzhed do/_/bdag gis ni rigs par ma mthong ste/_sems ma bzhis gnas bzhir byin gyis brlab par 'dod do/_/ji ltar sngon gyi sangs rgyas dang /_/sngags kyi tshul gyis

[\[16a.4\]]'ching ba 'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug thams cad rnam par chad nas skye bo lha dang mi phun sum tshogs par 'gyur ro/_/dmyal ba [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]byol song gi ngan song thams cad rnam par sbyangs nas tsha grang la sogs pa'i sdug bsngal mi bzad pa rnams rab tu zhi ste/_skye bo rnams bde ba dang

[\[16a.6\]]ba'i sbyor bas dkyil 'khor gyi lha rnams bskyed la/_lha de dag gi thugs kar yang sho d+ha ne'i sngags la sogs pa bkod la/_de dag kyang g.yo ba'i 'od zer dang ldan pa'o/_/de nas sngags de dag las 'od zer 'phros pas dkyil 'khor gyi lha gsal/_de nas bdag nyid kyi 
[\[16a.7\]]khong pa'i nang gsal bar 'gyur/_de nas thugs dpal be'u dang /_spyan dang mdzod spu dang /_ba spu'i bu ga nas ye shes kyi 'od zer 'phros pas stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams steng 'og min gyi gnas man chad nas/_'og dmyal ba tsha grang yan chad du khyab pas 'od 
[\[16b\]]
[\[16b.1\]]zer de las sngags ngan song gsum gzhom pa'i sngags rgyud phyi ma nas 'byung bas sems can rnams kyi nyon mongs pa'i bcing ba lngas bcings pa bkrol zhing bcad nas sku gsung thugs kyi sngags kyis lus ngag yid gsum sbyangs/_slar 'dus pas sems can thams cad bdag nyid kyi nye 'khor 
[\[16b.2\]]du bkug go/_/bkug nas gong gi byin gyis brlabs pa'i sngags kyis gnas nyi shu ru snod du rung bar byin gyis brlabs so/_/de nas khams gsum gyi sems can de dag nang sems kyi dkyil 'khor du bcug ste gnas so/_/de nas yang ye shes kyi 'od zer gong ma ltar 'phros pas ye shes kyi 
[\[16b.3\]]'od zer dang sku gsung thugs spyan drangs te bstims pas dbang bskur ba'i nus pa dang ldan par byas so/_/bdag nyid kyang de'i byin gyis brlabs so/_/de nas rig pa'i dbang dang /_rdo rje brtul zhugs dang /_dam tshig gsum dang /_rjes su gnang ba dang /_dbugs 
[\[16b.4\]]dbyung ba dang /_lung bstan pa dang /_rin po che sna bdun la sogs pa bskur bas sems can thams cad kyi bcings pa thams cad rnam par bcad do zhes pa la sogs pa'o/_/de nas phyir byung ste nor bu dri ma med pa'i steng du 'khod de/_kye ma sangs rgyas kye sangs rgyas/_/zhes bya ba'i bstod 
[\[16b.5\]]pas kyang bstod do/_/de nas nang sems kyi dkyil 'khor la bdag gis kyang mchod cing bstod de/_zhib tu mchog sdebs las shes par bya'o/_/des ni sbyor ba dang po dang nang sems kyi ting nge 'dzin bstan to/_/da ni dkyil 'khor rgyal mchog gi ting nge 'dzin ston te/_de ltar 
[\[16b.6\]]bdag bskyed nas dkyil 'khor rgyal mchog bstan to/_/de yang dang po stong pa'i don shes par bya ba dang /_'byung ba rim gyis bskyed nas b+h+rUM las gzhal yas khang slob dpon gyis las kyi rim pa las 'byung ba bzhin du bsams la/_de'i nang du seng ge'i gdan la sogs pa bsams te

[\[17a\]]
[\[17a.1\]]bya'o/_/de yang thugs ka nas zla ba'i dbus su/_oM sho d+ha ne sho d+ha ya sarba a pA ya sarba sa tA+e b+h+yaH/_hUM zhes pa'i sngags de rnams dbus nas shar phyogs su rim pas sngags kyi phreng ba'i tshul du bskor te/_phyogs bzhir bkod pa bsgom mo/_/de las 'od zer

[\[17a.4\]]nyid kyi ye shes kyi ngo bo'i skur gyur te/_bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ka nas bton nas bka' nod pa'i tshul du gnas pa las nam mkha' las byung ba'i rin po che'i dbu rgyan gyis dbang bskur ro/_/de nas rdo rje sems dpa' la sogs pas kyang byin gyis gnas bzhir brlab po/_/

[\[17a.6\]]bya'o/_/de yang kun du bzang po byang chub sems la gnas pa dang /_de dag gi 'phro 'du las dam tshig gi phyag rgyar gyur pa dang /_de la sku gsung thugs kyi dbyings su bsdu ba dang /_de nas so so'i skur gyur te dkyil 'khor du dgod pa dang /_de la dbang bskur ba dang /_byin gyis 
[\[17a.7\]]brlabs pa dang /_ming gdags shing las bcol ba ni gong ltar ro/_/oM sarba a pA ya bi sho d+ha ni hUM phaT ces pas spro ba dang bsdu ba las rdo rje pad+mar gyur to/_/de'i sku 'phro 'du las ni shAkya thub pa'i skur gyur to/_/de la yang byin gyis brlabs pa dang dbang bskur ba dang ming btags nas 
[\[17b\]]
[\[17b.1\]]nub kyi rdo rje pad+mas mtshan pa'i steng du dgod do/_/yang oM badzra traT ces pa'i sngags kyi yi ge las 'phro ba dang 'du ba las rdo rje rgya gram du gyur/_de'i 'phro 'du las brtan par gyur to/_/de'i 'phro 'du las me tog cher rgyas kyi skur gyur te/_bka' nod pa dang /_dbang bskur ba dang /_byin gyis

[\[17b.4\]]mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes la sogs pa'i ngo bo las byung ba thams cad seng ge'i gdan rin po che la sogs pa rigs kyi mtshan mas brgyan pa'i steng na bzhugs so/_/sngags de rnams ni bskyed pa'i rkyen yang yin la de dag thugs kar dgod pa yang yin la/_sku'i tsa ka li dgod pa yang

[\[18a.7\]]sogs pa'o/_/pha rol tu phyin pa las byung ba ni byams pa la sogs pa'o/_/de la ye shes sems dpa' rnams ni sgra yig la sogs pa las skyes pa'o/_/de la yang sgra yig ni/_a A i I u U e ai au au aM aHzhes pa la sogs pa'i sngags dang /_oM sarba bita sarba A ba ra 
[\[18b\]]
[\[18b.1\]]Na bi sho d+ha ya ha na hUM phaT ces pa la sogs pa dkyus na yod pa rnams so/_/de yang bcom ldan 'das kun rig rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs ka'i zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi dbus su sgra yig dang po a de'i mtha' ma na tshur oM badzra satwa hUM zhes pa'i sngags kyi phreng bas bskor ba'o/_/de'i phyi rim na tshur oM sarba

[\[18b.5\]]du sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pas 'gro ba'i don mdzad nas slar 'dus te rdo rje la thim pa las rdo rje sems dpa'i sku phyag rgya chen por gyur te/_bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ka na bzhugs so/_/yang de'i thugs kar zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la sngags snga ma bzhin du bkod de/_sngags de brjod de bskul

[\[19a\]]
[\[19a.1\]]la gdan sna tshogs pad+ma dang zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la bzhugs su gsol lo/_/de bzhin du 'og ma rnams kyang shes par bya ste/_dzaHdang hoHla sogs pa sngags kyi yi ge 'bru re re ba dang /_a la sogs pa bcu gcig dang /_/de'i phyi rim nas/_oM badzra rA dzA zhes bya

[\[19a.7\]]phyir ro/_/la la dag ni 'di bcu gnyis las zla ba bskyed la/_A li bcu gnyis las sems dpa' bcu drug bskyed la/_rtsa ba'i rig sngags ni dkyil 'khor du dgod la/_yi ge 'bru re la ni de dag gi snying gar zla ba'i steng du bsam mo zhes so/_/la la dag ni A li stong pa bcu drug 
[\[19b\]]
[\[19b.1\]]gi rang bzhin bas zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la/_yi ge 'bru re ba ni rang rang gi mtshan ma rdo rje la sogs pa bskyed la/_rtsa ba'i sngags badzra satwa la sogs pas ni skur gyur la/_bti bcu gnyis kyis ni lha de dag gi thugs kar zla ba'i steng na gnas nas ngan song la sogs pa sbyong ngo zhes 
[\[19b.2\]]gsungs so/_/stong pa bcu drug dang sems dpa' dang /_dbyangs yig tu sbyar ba ni 'og nas 'byung bas 'dir ma bris te/_ci'i phyir zhe na/[ {]mangs [,]mang [}]pas skrag pa'i phyir ro/_/bita ni rig pa yin la rig pa ni ye shes te/_ye shes las bskyed pa ni sems dpa' bcu drug go/_/de yang rgyu'i sgo 
[\[19b.3\]]nas 'bras bu yang de bzhin te/_sems dpa' bskyed pa'i sngags la yang bita rig pa zhes gdags so/_/'o na bskal bzang nas 'gro ba rigs drug yan chad kun ni rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs las skye na zhe'o/_de yang bden te thugs ye shes las skyes pa yang 'dir dkyil 
[\[19b.4\]]'khor gyi dbang du bya ste thams cad 'byung du 'dra na yang sems dpa' la gnyis te/_sngags kyi ye shes las skyes pa'i ye shes sems dpa' dang /_pha rol tu phyin pa'i sems dpa'o/_/'on kyang bita bcu gnyis ni sngags kyi sems dpar nges so/_/sngags kyi sems dpa' de dag 
[\[19b.5\]]la gnas bzhir byin gyis brlab cing /_ye shes chen po lnga'i dbu rgyan gyi dbang bskur bar bya'o/_/da ni yum bzhi bskyed par bya ste/_de yang rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs ka'i zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la yi ge ya ra la wa bzhi bkod pa'i mtha' man tshur satwa badzri/_rat+na badzri/_
[\[19b.6\]]d+harma badzri/_karma badzri/_zhes sngags kyi phreng ba bkod pa'i 'phro 'du las dam tshig gi phyag rgya rdo rje dang /_rin po che dang /_pad+ma dang /_rgya gram dang bzhir gyur to/_/de'i 'phro 'du las yum bzhi'i sku bskyed de/_spyan ma dang /_mA ma kI dang /_gos dkar mo dang /_sgrol ma bzhir gyur te 
[\[19b.7\]]thugs ka na gnas so/_/yang de dag gi so so'i thugs kar rang rang gi sngags kyi yi ge'i phreng ba bkod nas sngags brjod de bskul ba'i tshul gyis phyi rol du 'phros te/_sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa mdzad nas slar 'dus pa nyid la byin gyis brlabs pa dang /_dbang bskur/_dam tshig gi phyag rgya lag 
[\[20a\]]
[\[20a.1\]]tu byin te/_ming gi dbang bskur nas las la bzhag ste nang gi re'u mig gi gra bzhir bzhugs so/_/kha cig na re yum bzhi po 'di dag ni rtsa ba'i rgyud chen po las kyang sku'i phyag rgya ma byung ste/_dam tshig gi phyag rgyar ram/_rang gi rigs kyi mtshan mar te/_/'di 
[\[20a.2\]]nyid las kyang /_/sangs rgyas spyan dang mA ma kI/_/de bzhin gos dkar sgrol ma rnams/_/rang gi rigs kyi mtshan ma bzhin/_/grwa yi phyogs ni kun du bri/_/zhes gsungs so/_/nang gi lha mo rnams ni yi ge sha las ni sgeg mo ma'o/_/yi ge Sha las ni phreng ba ma'o/_/yi ge sa yis 
[\[20a.3\]]ni dbyangs mkhan ma'o/_/yi ge ha yis ni gar mkhan ma'o/_/yi ge k+Sha yis ni spos me ma'o/_/yi ge ra yis ni me tog ma'o/_/yi ge la yis ni mar me ma'o/_/yi ge wa yis ni dri chab ma'o/_/yi ge tsa yis ni lcags kyu ma'o/_/yi ge tsha yis ni zhags pa ma'o/_/yi ge dza 
[\[20a.4\]]yis ni lcags sgrog ma'o/_/yi ge dz+ha yis ni dril bu ma'o/_/ngan song sbyong ba'i sngags ring las ni byams pa nas/_'gro ba rigs drug yan chad bskyed par bya ste rang gi rtsa ba'i sngags dbus so/_/de rnams las ji lta bar dbyibs dang kha dog bskyed par bya ste rdul tshon du'o/_/des ni dkyil 
[\[20a.5\]]'khor rgyal po mchog gi ting nge 'dzin bstan to/_/da ni phan yon bstan te/_de la gnyis te/_gson po la ji ltar byas na phan yon che ba dang /[ (]tsha'i [,]tshe'i [)]dus byas pa la ji ltar byas na phan yon che ba bstan te/_lha'i dbang po zhes bya ba la sogs pa gsungs te/_phyi ma dkyus na gsal 
[\[20a.6\]]bas ma bris so/_/dus ma yin par 'chi ba brgyad ni sa gnyan dral ba dang /_bud med rgan mo dang nyal ba dang /_mi na bzhin du sman za ba dang /_khong steng na nyal ba dang /_gdon 'byung po yod pa'i sa phyogs su gcig pur 'gro ba dang /_nad pas sman pa'i ngag mi nyan pa dang /_
[\[20a.7\]]'thab mo'i g.yul du 'jug pa dang /_mi thub pa la sogs pa dgrar len pa ni dus ma yin par 'chi ba'o/_/dus ma yin par 'chi ba dang 'brel pa ni/_chu chen po brgal ba dang /_rbab sar 'dug pa dang /_mtshon la rtse ba dang /_dug 'thung ba rnams ni 'chi ba dang 'brel pa'o/_/
[\[20b\]]
[\[20b.1\]]rmi lam gyi rnam pa ni sum cu rtsa gcig ste/_gang zhe na/_dgras ded pa dang /_skyabs dang bral ba dang /_sdug bsngal gyis gzir ba dang /[ {]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}(]gyis [,]kyis [)]mthu 'phrogs pa dang /_mig phyung ba dang /_kha nas lce 'phrogs pa dang /_nyi zla lhung ba dang /_sa gzhi mi gtsang bas gang ba dang /_
[\[20b.2\]]skra byi ba dang /_sgren mor 'dam du 'das pa dang /_gzhan gyis mgor phog pa dang /_lcags su bcug pa dang /_dug 'thung ba dang /_mi gtsang bas lus byugs pa dang /_khyim la me shor ba dang /_mi gtsang ba'i sbubs su lhung ba dang /_mi bsngags pa'i zhal lces 'debs pa dang /_sgra mi snyan pa 
[\[20b.3\]]grags pa dang /_grogs dang bral ba dang /_chus khyer ba dang /_mgo la me shor ba dang /_rgyal po'i chad pas go rar bcug pa dang /_bdog pa gzhan gyis phrogs pa dang /_gcan gzan mchu can gyis sha za ba dang /_dmyal bar lhung ba dang /_mun nag la 'dug pa dang /_ngo tsha ba dang /
[\[20b.4\]]mya ngan gyi nang na 'dug pa dang /_bdud kyi zhags pas bzung ba dang /_srin pos khyer ba dang /_phongs pa'i mi rnams shi ba dang /_gzhan gyi 'tshe bas 'degs pa'o/_/phyi ma dkyus la gsal/_da ni sems can tshe'i dus byas pa ngan song du skyes na/_dkyil 'khor du bcug nas 
[\[20b.5\]]dbang bskur na thar pa'i thabs ston te/_skyes pa'am zhes bya ba la sogs pa 'byung ste/_shi ba'i lus la gnas nyi shu ru gong gi sngags kyis byin gyis brlabs nas dbang bskur na yon tan 'di rnams thob par 'gyur ro zhes te/_dkyus na gsal lo/_/da ni rang bzhin gyi dkyil 'khor 
[\[20b.6\]]bstan te/_de la yang zhus pa dang /_zhus pa'i lan gnyis so/_/de nas lha rnams kyi dbang po zhes bya ba nas bshad du gsol zhes bya ba'i bar gyis zhus pa ston te/_dkyus gsal lo/_/de nas zhus pa'i lan gsungs te/_de nas bcom ldan 'das zhes bya ba la sogs pa'o/_/de nas 
[\[20b.7\]]zhes bya ba ni dkyil 'khor bshad pa'i rjes thogs la bya'o/_/bcom ldan 'das ni dbang phyug la sogs pa'i yon tan dang ldan pas bcom ldan 'das so/_/shAkya ni de'i rigs su skyes shing sku gsung thugs bsrung ba gsum dang ldan pas thub pa ste/_dran pa nye bar gzhag pa gsum gyi phyir ro/_/
[\[21a\]]
[\[21a.1\]]ji ltar song ba de bzhin gshegs pas na/_de bzhin gshegs pa'o/_/ngan song ni bshad zin/_ye shes rdo rje zhes bya ba ni ye shes ni rnam par mi rtog pa'o/_/de las byung ba'i ye shes chen po lnga la ni rdo rje zhes bya'o/_/de bas ni de bzhin gshegs pa de dag gi nang sems 
[\[21a.2\]]kyi dkyil 'khor rang bzhin med pa de la ni chen po zhes bya'o/_/'di ni blo la gnas pa gsungs so/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi byang chub sems/_/zhes bya ba la sogs pa la/_sangs rgyas kun gyi zhes bya ba ni 'khor ba dang mya ngan las 'das pa'i chos thams cad kyi bde bar gshegs pa rnams kyi bdag 
[\[21a.3\]]po bde bar gshegs pa lnga rnams gcig tu gyur pa/_bde bar gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku rtag pa dang /_kun gzhi rnam par shes pa rnam par dag pa me long lta bu'i ye shes mi 'khrugs pa dang /_nyon mongs pa can gyi yid rnam par dag pa mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes rin chen 'byung 
[\[21a.4\]]ba dang /_yid kyi rnam par shes pa rnam par dag pa so sor kun rtog pa'i ye shes 'od dpag tu med pa dang /_sgo lnga'i rnam par shes pa rnam par dag pa bya ba nan tan gyi ye shes don yod grub pa rnams so/_/'o na bde bar gshegs pa ni mtha' yas pa'i grangs yod na/_'di lnga 
[\[21a.5\]]la kun zhes bya ba mi 'gal lam zhe na/_de bden te/_de bzhin gshegs pa mtha' yas pa'i phyir de'i rigs kyang mtha' yas pa ste/_de dag kyang bsdu na rigs lngar 'dus te/_de la rgyud las gsungs pa/_mtha' yas rigs rnams thams cad kyang /_/rigs ni rnam pa 
[\[21a.6\]]brgyar bshad pa/_/de bzhin gshegs dang rdo rje dang /_/pad+ma rin chen las kyi rigs/_/mdor bsdus na ni rnam pa lnga/_/zhes gsungs te/_de yang ci'i phyir zhe na/_ye shes lnga kho na dbang gis bsdus na rgyal ba yang rigs lnga kho na'o/_/de ltar sangs rgyas ma lus pa de 
[\[21a.7\]]dag gi byang chub sems ni gang zhe na/_de ni sbyong ba'i rgyud 'di las rgyu'i ye shes byang chub kyi sems rnam pa gnyis dang /_'bras bu gsum ste lnga yin pas/_de ni gzhan gyi byang chub las mchog tu gyur pa'i phyir na bla na med pa zhes bya'o/_/de lta bu'i byang chub kyi sems de su'i yul yin 
[\[21b\]]
[\[21b.1\]]zhe na/_rtog gyur pa zhes bya ste/_bla zhes bya ba ni mdun du'am sngon du zhes thal ba la bsnyag par bya'o/_/de yang rgyud 'di'i sngon du ni rtsa ba'i rgyud de kho na nyid bsdus pa byung ba las byang chub sems dpa' don thams cad grub pas rtogs par gyur pa gang yin pa'o/_/gang 
[\[21b.2\]]rtogs par gyur na byang chub kyi sems lnga'o/_/gang gis rtogs par byas she na/_rgyal bas zhes bya ste/_sngon du sangs rgyas pa'i rgyal ba de rnams kyis so/_/gang du rtogs par byas na byang chub kyi snying por ro/_/de yang sngags kyi sgor spyod pa'i sems dpa' rnams ni sngags kyi 
[\[21b.3\]]don slob dpon la rag las pa yin gyis/_rang gi thug thub tu spyad pa ni mi rung ba'i phyir don thams cad grub pa ni byang chub sems dpa'i byang chub ni sangs rgyas snga ma la rag las pa ltar snang ngo /_/de'i phyir rigs bzhi'i sangs rgyas de dag gis btul zhing dge ba'i bshes gnyen mdzad 
[\[21b.4\]]pa las dgongs te/_'dir yang byang chub kyi sems de brtan par bya ba'i phyir gsungs zhes bya'o/_/pa'i zhes bya ba'i don yang sngon du song ba la snyegs pa'i don te/_'di yang sngon du dpal de kho na nyid bsdus pa'i rgyud du don thams cad grub pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
[\[21b.5\]]chen po stug po bkod pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing byang chub kyi snying po na bzhugs pa las sngon gyi sangs rgyas kyis mngon par byang chub pa lnga bsngags shing phul ba zhes bya'o/_/ye shes gang zhes bya ba ni ma log pa'i stong pa nyid kyi ting nge 'dzin la sbyor ba las/_yul so sor rtog par rig pas 
[\[21b.6\]]rang gi sems rig pa stong pa chen po dang /_gnyis pa byang chub tu sems bskyed pa me long ye shes dang /_gsum pa chos kyi skur gnas pa so sor rtog pa'i ye shes dang /_bzhi pa brtan par byas pa mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes dang /_lnga pa skur gyur pa bya ba nan tan gyi ye shes zhes bya'o/_/
[\[21b.7\]]yang slob dpon la la dag gi bzhed lugs kyis byang chub kyi sems rnam pa lnga las rang gi sems so sor rig pa dang /_byang chub kyi sems skyes pa zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gnyis rgyu'i ye shes zhes bya ste/_de la ci'i phyir rgyu'i ye shes zhes bya zhe na/_byang chub kyi sems dang po ni sa dang po rab tu dga' ba'i 
[\[22a\]]
[\[22a.1\]]mtshan nyid de/_mthong ba'i lam gyi ye shes kyi rang bzhin no/_/de'i 'og ma byang chub kyi sems ni sa gnyis pa nas sa bcu pa'i bar gyi ye shes te/_bsgom pa'i lam mo/_/de'i phyir de gnyis ni rgyu'i ye shes 'phags pa'i lam gyi rang bzhin no/_/de ltar rgyu'i ye shes 
[\[22a.2\]]te zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi steng du 'bras bu'i ye shes rdo rje rtse lnga pa bsgoms te/_ci'i phyir ye shes lnga la 'bras bu zhes bya zhe na/_ye shes lnga ni chos kyi sku/_chos kyi sku de yang rnal 'byor pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi don dang /_sku gnyis 'byung ba la mthu yod par bya ba'i phyir brtag par bya'o/_/
[\[22a.3\]]gzhan gyi don phun sum tshogs par bsgrub par bya ba'i phyir ni longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku phyag rgya chen por gyur pa'o/_/de ltar byang chub sems dpa'i bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs ma lus pa thams cad ni yongs su rdzogs pa'i 'bras bu dpag bsam gyi shing dang /_yid 
[\[22a.4\]]bzhin gyi nor bu rin po che lta bu'i ngo bo kun du bzang po byang chub kyi sems zhes bya ba byang chub kyi sems bla na med pa rdo rje ltar sra zhing brtan pa/_cis kyang mi shig cing 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa thams cad kyi sems kyi rgyal po rang gi sems byin gyis brlabs 
[\[22a.5\]]nas brtan pa'i ye shes gang yin pa de ni sbyor ba'i rgyud kyi dkyil 'khor thams cad kyi yang snying por de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gsungs zhes bya'o/_/rang bzhin gyi dkyil 'khor de'i don yang 'di ltar snyegs te/_dang por yon tan bcu dang ldan pas gnas yid du 'ong bar 
[\[22a.6\]]khrus la sogs pa bya ste/_stan bde ba la 'dug nas sngon du 'gro ba'i yan lag byas nas/_rdo rje sems dpa'i skur bdag nyid/_/chos rnams thams cad dngos med par/_/sems kyi bsgom par byas pas ni/_/a las zla ba'i dkyil 'khor bsam/_/zhes bya ba la sogs 
[\[22a.7\]]pa gsungs pa'i don gyis bsams la/_de la byin gyis brlabs pa dang /_dbang dang mchod pa yan chad byas nas/_nang sems kyi dkyil 'khor skad cig mas bskyed nas ngan song nas drang zhing 'gugs pa la yan man gyi dbang gis rnam par grol ba'i lam la bzhag nas/_des ngo 
[\[22b\]]
[\[22b.1\]]mtshar gyi stong pa nyid gzhal yas khang bskyed pa dang gdan bsams nas gong gi mngon par byang chub pa lngas bdag rnam par snang mdzad du 'og min du sangs rgyas pa'i tshul gyis sangs rgyas nas rin po che dbang bskur ba dang /_spyan ras gzigs kyi chos kyi ye shes bskyed nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
[\[22b.2\]]phrin las la bzhag ste/_seng ge'i khri'i steng du 'dug ste/_dkyil 'khor rgyal po mchog ltar lha rnams sku bskyed pa dang /_'og nas 'byung ba'i las rgyal mchog ltar las rnams byas nas/_de nas las la rjes su sbyor ba'i spyan drang ba dang /_bzhugs su gsol ba dang /_phyag rgya bzhis 
[\[22b.3\]]rgyas gdab pa dang /_dbang bskur ba dang /_byin gyis brlabs shing mchod pa dang /_sngags kyi don la gnas pa yan chad byas nas rdo rje'i bzlas pa bya ste/_bdag kun rig rnam par snang mdzad kyi skur gsal bar byas pa las/_de'i thugs kar rnam par snang mdzad chen po bsams te/
[\[22b.4\]]sangs rgyas de'i thugs kar shes rab kyi 'khor lo bsgom par bya ste/_ci 'dra ba zhe na/_rdo rje rtse mo'i rgyud las rten cing 'brel bar 'byung ba'i don gsungs pa/_rtsibs kyi lte ba yang dag 'dzin/_/rtsibs rnams lte ba la gnas pa/_/rten cing 'brel bar 'byung sbyor bas/_/
[\[22b.5\]]'khor lor 'di ni gyur pa yin/_/zhes so/_/yi ge b+h+rUM las bskyed pa'i 'khor lo rtsibs bcu drug pa mu khyud bzhi dang ldan pa shin tu bkod pa'i lte ba de'i dkyil du yi ge hUM bsams te/_bcom ldan 'das kun rig gi rtsa ba'i sngags la sogs pa bsam par bya ste/_yi ge'i kha 
[\[22b.6\]]dog ni dkar ba bsam mo/_/de la 'khor lo'i lte ba la re'u mig dgur byas pa la kun rig rnam par snang mdzad la sogs pa'i sngags dang /_yum gyi sngags rnams dgod do/_/de nas dang po'i mu khyud la oM sho d+ha ni sho d+ha ni sarba pA paM bi sho d+ha ni shud+d+he bi shud+d+he sarba karma A 
[\[22b.7\]]ba ra Na bi shud+d+he swA hA/_zhes pa'o/_/de'i phyi rim mu khyud gnyis pa la/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa bcu gnyis brjod pa sgra'i yi ge bcu gnyis bsgom par bya'o/_/de nas dang po lce nas brtsams nas ji tsam du bita bcu gnyis bkod pa ni rigs kyi mdog dang sbyar ro/_/
[\[23a\]]
[\[23a.1\]]de'i phyi rim gyi mu khyud la rtsa ba'i sngags oM na mo b+ha ga ba ti sarba durga ti zhes bya ba'i sngags ring pos bskor ba'o/_/phyi rol gyi mu khyud gnyis pa la ka kha la sogs pa nas ha'i mthar thug pa'i yi ge dgod par bya'o/_/de ni 'khor lo'i sngags dgod pa'i rim 
[\[23a.2\]]pa'o/_/'khor lo'i dbus na gnas pa'i yi ge hUM las rdo rje/_rdo rje'i lte ba la thams cad mkhyen pa'i rang bzhin thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes 'byung ba'i rkyen/_nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas thams cad skyed pa'i dngos po byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi bsod nams dang 
[\[23a.3\]]ye shes kyi dngos po'o/_/don dam pa'i yig 'bru tshig 'bru thams cad kyi rkyen gyi dngos po yi ge hUM dgod do/_/de nas de las 'od byung bas nang gi sems kyi dkyil 'khor gsal lo/_/de nas mi [(]mnged [,]mjed [)]kyi 'jig rten gyi khams gsal lo/_de nas nam mkha'i khams thams cad du khyab 
[\[23a.4\]]par sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam pa sna tshogs pa rnams su song nas ma lus pa'i sems can gyi don byas nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang /_byang chub sems dpa' dang nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi 'khor yang snang bar byas te/_slar ye shes kyi rdo rje lan gsum bskor nas 
[\[23a.5\]]shes rab kyi ye shes de drangs te chos bdag med pa nyid kyis rang gi sgo nas btsud nas rang gi snying ga'i zla ba'i dkyil na gnas pa'i rdo rje dang hUM la thim nas gnas par bsgom par bya'o/_/de ltar rgyun du bsgoms pa la chags pa'i rnal 'byor pa sngags kyi sgo nas spyad pa spyod pa'i 
[\[23a.6\]]byang chub sems dpa' bcom ldan 'das kun rig rnam par snang mdzad kyi sku'i sgo nas dmigs so zhes pa dang /_zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi dbyibs kyi sgo nas de'i gzugs rjes su bsam mo zhes pa dang /_yi ge hUM gi gzugs kyi sgo nas de'i gzugs rjes su bsgom mo zhes so/_/
[\[23a.7\]]de ltar rnam pa gsum gyi tshul gyis bsgoms pas de nyid sprul pa'i gzugs kyi skus dus ring mo ma yin pa [(]kha[,] kho [)]nar 'dul ba'i dbang nye bar bzung ste mngon du byas so/_/nam mkha' bzhi la gnas pa'i sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi sprul pa'i gzugs kyi sku 
[\[23b\]]
[\[23b.1\]]dang lhan cig tu gnas pa yang mngon du bya'o zhes so/_/de ltar bsgom pa'i dbang de las tshe 'di kho na la sangs rgyas nyid kyi rdo rje 'dzin pa nyid 'thob bo zhes so/_/de ltar rim pa 'dis ngan song sbyong ba'i rdzogs pa'i rim pa gsungs pa yin no/_/de nas sprul pa'i sku shAkya thub pa'i sangs 
[\[23b.2\]]rgyas kun gyis byang chub kyi sems gsungs te/_de la re zhig dang po'i sbyor ba kun sngon du byas nas/_shAkya thub pa'i thugs kar yi ge hrIHlas bskyed pa'i snang ba mtha' yas sku mdog dmar po ting nge 'dzin gyi phyag rgya dang ldan pa bsgoms te de'i thugs kar lce brgyad pa'i 'khor lo'i mu 
[\[23b.3\]]khyud bzhi dang ldan pa rtsa ba'i sngags la sogs pas yongs su bskor ba bsgom par bya'o/_/de nas lce brgyad pa la sa bon rnams ni oM sarba A ba ra Na bi sho d+ha na badzra hUM phaT ces pa la sogs pa bkod de/_'khor lo'i lte ba'i dbus na yi ge hrIHlas gyur pa'i pad+ma rdo rjes mtshan pa 
[\[23b.4\]]bsam mo/_/de la yi ge hrIHsa mtshan pa'o/_/spro ba dang bsdu ba gong ma bzhin du rim pas byas nas bsgom par bya'o/_/de kho na bzhin dang po'i sbyor ba byas nas dkyil 'khor gyi dbus su rdo rje sems dpa' de'i thugs kar bcom ldan 'das mi bskyod pa sku mdog sngon po sa gnon gyi phyag rgya 
[\[23b.5\]]can bsgoms nas/_de'i thugs kar 'khor lo rtsibs bzhi pa mu khyud bzhi dang ldan pa bsams la/_mu khyud dang po la oM puN+ye puN+ye zhes bya ba la sogs pa'o/_/lce bzhi la/_oM a mi te a mi ta a mi tod b+ha be zhes bya ba la sogs pa'o/_/de'i lte bar rdo rje hUM gis mtshan pa'o/_/
[\[23b.6\]]de kho na bzhin du spro ba la sogs pa bya'o/_/yang de kho na bzhin du yi ge b+h+rUM las bskyed pa'i rnam par snang mdzad de'i thugs kar rnam par snang mdzad chen po bsams te/_de'i thugs kar 'khor lo rtsibs bzhi pa cig bsams te/_oM trAM swA hA zhes bya ba la sogs pa bkod

[\[24a\]]
[\[24a.1\]]rin chen 'byung ba'am/_rnam par snang mdzad gang yang rung ba bsgoms la/_de'i thugs kar 'khor lo rtsibs bzhi pa bkod la/_'khor lo la badzra hUM phaT ces pa la sogs pa bkod la/_dbus su rigs kyi phyag rgya gang yin pa bsams la/_de las 'od 
[\[24a.2\]]zer spro ba dang bsdu ba gong ma ltar ro/_/rdo rje nyi ma'i ni logs na'o/_/de yan chad kyis ni/_sangs rgyas kun gyis byang chub sems/_/zhes bya ba la sogs pa'i bskyed pa dang rdzogs pa'i rang bzhin gyi dkyil 'khor bstan to/_/da 'di yan chad ni slob dpon gyi las kyi rim pa'i cho ga rgyas pa bshad 
[\[24a.3\]]de/_de la yang dkyil 'khor bri ba dang /_lha la sogs pa dgod pa dang /_mchod pa gsum gyis bstan pa ste/_de la yang dkyil 'khor bri ba ston te/_de la yang tshon dgye ba dang /_byin gyis brlabs pa'o/_/de yang byin gyis brlabs pa ston te/_de la yang sngon du 'gro ba'i 
[\[24a.4\]]yan lag bsnyen pa bstan te/_dam tshig dang /_dbang bskur ba thob pas zla ba gcig gam lo gcig gi bar du rig pa'i brtul zhugs spyad par bya'o/_/de la dang por khrus byas la de nas phyag 'tshal ba la sogs pa sngon du 'gro bas nyi ma re re zhing sdom pa bzung ba yang lan drug 
[\[24a.5\]]tu bya'o/_/de nas sdig pa dral ba dang /_phyag rgya thams cad 'grol ba ji lta ba bzhin du byas la rnal 'byor chen po bya'o/_/de nas snying gar dkyil 'khor ji lta ba bzhin bsgom par bya'o/_/yang na rnal 'byor chen po dang /_dang po sbyor ba dang /_dkyil 'khor rgyal po 
[\[24a.6\]]mchog rnam par bsgoms nas/_bsdu ba bya ba dang /_phyag 'tshal ba dang /_ched du brjod pa dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la phreng ba'i dbang bskur ba la sogs pa thams cad blangs la de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad rdo rje lcags kyu la sogs pas dgug pa dang /_
[\[24a.7\]]gzhug pa dang /_bcing pa dang /_dbang du byas nas dam tshig gi phyag rgya rnams kyis bsgrub par bya'o/_/de nas chos dang las dang /_phyag rgya chen po rnams kyis rgyas btab pa dang dbang bskur te las rnams la nges par sbyar bar bya'o/_/de la dang po re zhig kun rig rnam par snang mdzad kyi 
[\[24b\]]

[\[28b.1\]]tshogs rdo rje rnams bkod kyang rung ngo /_/de yang /_mu stegs can la phan gdags phyir/_/sangs rgyas btsun mo rab tu gsang /_/zhes so/_/da ni ye shes sems dpa' rdo rje sems dpa' la sogs pa dgod pa ston te/_rtsibs kyi nang ni thams cad du/_/zhes bya ba la sogs pa gsungs te/_

[\[31a.1\]]khro bo mang po 'byung bas pho nya ste/_de'i phyir na brgyad de/_'jig rten gsum snang dang /_'jig rten gsum 'ching dang /_'jig rten gsum 'jigs dang /_'jig rten mthar byed dang /_bdud rtsi thab sbyor dang /_be con sngon po dang /_mi g.yo mgon po dang /_rta mgrin te de rnams ni shar

[\[32a.1\]]log 'dren nag po chu ltar g.yo ba sprul te/_gnye'u thung rna rnon me/_spyi'u tshugs thur blta bya/_se'u men ta ga snying du 'jug pa'i 'dre dang /_dar dpyangs de ta ka lce la 'jug pa dang /_mur rgad ta ka myur bar 'jug pa ral gri bsnams pa dang drug go/_klu dang

[\[32a.4\]]rigs rnams dang zhes pa ni phyogs skyong ba bcu ni bgegs kyi rigs su gtogs pas rigs rnams so/_/brgya byin rdo rje/_gshin rje thod dbyug_/chu lha sbrul zhags/_gnod sbyin be con/_me lha bgrang phreng /_bden bral ral gri/_rlung lha ba dan/_dbang ldan rtse gsum/_sa

[\[33a.1\]]dang /_gnon po dang /_skem byed dang /_grib gnon dang /_ma mo'i gdon dang /_bi na ya ga btsun mo dang bcas pa ste brgyad do/_/sems can kun la phan sems pa'i/_/drang srong skal ba chen po rnams/_/zhes pa ni drang srong ni brgyad de/_me bzhi rin po che 'bar ba bsnams

[\[36b.1\]]rang gi gnas su gzhug par bya'o/_/de nyid las mdzub mo bsdams pa ni lcags sgrog gi tshul lo/_/des ni phyag rgya thams cad bcing bar bya'o/_/de nyid las mthe bo gnyis nang du bkug nas phyag rgya bskor ba ni dril bu'i phyag rgya ste/_rnam par snang mdzad la sogs pa dbang du bya'o/_/de nas rdo rje gnod sbyin

[\[36b.3\]]'dis bsgrub par bya'o/_/kun rig rnam par snang mdzad kyi dam tshig gi phyag rgya bcings la 'di skad ces brjod do/_/sho d+ha ni'i sngags kyi rjes la badzra d+hA tu dr-i sh+ya dzaHhU~M baM hoHsa ma ya s+t+waM/_sa ma ya s+t+waM a haM badzra d+hA tu zhes bya ba nas/_de bzhin du rdo rje 'bebs pa'i dam tshig gi phyag rgya bcings la/

[\[82b.1\]]gun+d+he dang /_zhugs gling bsnams pa'o/_/lhor gshin rje nag po ma he'i gdan la bzhugs pa phyag na thod dbyug bsnams pa'o/_/lho nub tu bden bral dmar nag [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]kyi gdan la bzhugs pa ral gri thogs pa'o/_/nub tu chu bdag mdog dmar sbrul gyi gdengs ka can sbrul zhags

[\[83a.1\]]par bde ba'i stan nang tshangs can la 'dug ste/_dang por bgegs bskrad pa dang /_/rdo rje sems dpa' 'am 'jig rten gsum las rnam par rgyal ba mdun du dmigs nas mchod pa dang /_phyag 'tshal ba dang /_sdig pa bshags pa dang /_skyabs su 'gro ba dang /_/

[\[83a.3\]]bi dra ba bi dra ba/_sarba a ba ya nA sha ya nA sha ya/_ha ra ha ra prA NAM hU~M zhes brjod pas khro bo khams gsum rnam par rgyal ba'i skur gyur te/_sku mdog sngon po zhal bgrad cing mche ba gtsigs pa phyag na rdo rje dang /_pad+ma dang /_ral gri dang /_be con bsnams pa/_sbrul gyis brgyan pa/_stag lpags kyi 
[\[83a.4\]]smad brgyan pa/_'khor khro bo mang pos bskor ba/_rigs lngas dbu brgyan pa/_zhabs pad+ma lta bus ro mnan nas/_thabs mkhas pa'i thugs rjes ngan song sbyang bar mdzad pa bsam mo/_/de nas gzhal yas khang ni gru bzhi pa sgo bzhi pa/_rta babs dang mu khyud du bcas pa nang dkyil 'khor dgu pa 
[\[83a.5\]]snam bu rim pa gnyis pa bsgom mo/_/de'i dbus su gnas par bya ste/_'khor gza' chen po rnams rim par bskyed par bya'o/_/de la dbus kyi gtso bo'i thugs kar zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi steng du oM A zhes pas sngags bskyed par bya'o/_/de'i 'phro 'du las pad+mar gyur te/_de'i 'phro 'du las 
[\[83a.6\]]gza' nyi ma'i skur bskyed de/_sku mdog dmar ser la gser gyi ya lad dam 'thab ber gsol ba phyag na pad+ma bsnams pa'o/_/de'i snying gar oM A di t+ya swA hA/_zhes bkod pa de brjod pa'i tshul gyis phyir phyung ste/_rlung gi phyogs su rta'i steng du bya'o/_/yang dbus kyi gtso bo'i thugs kar zla

[\[83b.1\]]dbyug pa dang /_sdigs mdzub bam zla ba rnams su gyur to/_/de rnams kyi 'phro 'du las so sor sku rnams su gyur to/_/de nas lha rnams kyi thugs kar oM swa moM swA hA/_oM bu d+ha swA hA/_oM bri ha ya swA hA/_oM shU raM ga me swA hA/_oM sa naish+tsa ra swA hA/_oM rA hu la swA hA/_zhes pa bsams

[\[85a.1\]]ces bya ba'i sgras zhes pas ni de nas zhes bya ba ni gza' chen po brgyad kyi dkyil 'khor bstan pa de nas so/_/klu chen po brgyad ni 'og nas 'byung ba'i grangs so/_/phuT ces bya ba'i sgras zhes pa ni klu chen po brgyad kyi snying po spyir dril ba ste/_klu thams cad de las mi 'da'

[\[85b\]]
[\[85b.1\]]kyi pad+ma'i 'dab ma la bkod do/_/de yang dbus kyi gtso bo'i thugs kar zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi steng du rtsa ba'i sngags/_oM phU/_oM phU/_oM pho/_oM phI/_oM phai/_oM phai/_oM phai/_zhes pa la/_oM tak+Sha kA ya swA hA/_oM karko TA ya swA hA/_oM ku li kA ya swA hA/_oM bA su ki ye

[\[85b.7\]]bzhi ni gzugs sna tshogs la mngar gsum dang /_klu sman sna tshogs kyis mchod pa'o/_/de yang sbrul mgo'i phyag rgya dang /_dzaHhU~M baM hoHzhes pas spyan drang ngo /_/de nas phyag rgya bstan nas dam la gnas par byas te mchod/_de nas bstod pas bstod do/_/phuT ces pa'i snying po nus

[\[86a.1\]]tshad du bzlas so/_/de nas 'khor rigs bzhi spyan drangs la gzugs la bstims te/_bya khyung gi phyag rgyas bsdigs la gdan la phab ste/_mngar gsum dang klu sman sna tshogs pas mchod do/_/de nas dkyil 'khor du klu'i rgyal po la sogs pa bcug nas phuT

[\[86a.7\]]'dzag pa ni chu'i khams shas che ba'i dug yin pas dkyil 'khor dang 'od dkar por gtor ro/_/kha skams pa dang khrag 'bros pa ni me'i khams kyi shas che ba'i dug yin pas sbrul dang 'od nag pos bya'o/_/skam zhing lce bskyod pa dang /_mdog ngan par 'dug pa dang /_nag por

[\[87a.1\]]pa'i rim par shes par bya ste/_de yang dang po sbyor ba dgos/_/sngon du 'gro ba'i las rnams byas nas rdo rje thal mo dang rdo rje bcings pa dang /_ye shes dbab pa dang /_brtan pa la sogs pa byas nas/_de nas rang gi sems so sor brtag pa dang /_cir yang

[\[87a.4\]]stod g.yogs pa dang /_ba ta'i smad g.yogs can rgyan kun gyis brgyan pa/_zhal gsum pa/_g.yas zhi ba/_g.yon khro bo/_dbus ma yang phyed khro bo phyed zhi ba/_khro 'dzum mche ba lhag ge byung ba bsgoms la/_de nyid la cho 'phrul chen po bzhi dang ldan par byin gyis brlabs nas

[\[87a.7\]]ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi steng du oM b+hai ra ba swA hA/_oM b+ha bi swA hA/_oM b+ha swA hA/_oM b+hi swA hA/_oM b+hu swA hA/_oM b+hai swA hA/_oM b+hai swA hA/_oM b+ho swA hA/_oM b+haM swA hA/_oM b+haHswA hA/_zhes bya ba'i sngags rnams rim gyis bkod pa la/_phyag mtshan kha T+wAM ga dang /_pad+ma dang /_/

[\[87b.1\]]'khor lo dang /_mdung rtse gsum pa dang /_rdo rje gdengs pa dang /_mda' gzhu dang /_dgra sta dang /_snyim pa bkan te brkyang ba dang /_mdung thung rnams su gyur to/_/de las spro ba dang /_bsdu bas sku phyag rgyar gyur te gnas pa las rang rang gi thugs kar zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi steng

[\[88a.1\]]de dag kyang khros pa'i cha byad do/_/phyi rol gyi gru chad la dus bzhi bri/_sgo bzhir yang sgo skyong khros pa'i cha byad du/_dzaHhU~M baM hoHlas bskyed do/_/de nas ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor dang /_'jigs byed chen po brgyad spyan drangs te/_gnyis su med par byas nas phyag rgya rnams

[\[90b.1\]]Tha yis ni legs gnas so/_/Da yis ni brtson pa'o/_/D+ha yis ni rgyal ba'o/_/pa yis ni nor sbyin no/_/pha yis ni chu lha'o/_/ba yis ni me'o/_/b+ha yis ni ku be ra'o/_/ra yis ni phag mgo can no/_/zla tshes dang thig ler ldan pas ni gshin rje'o/_/ka yis ni chu'o/_/kha yis ni tshogs bdag

[\[91b.1\]]pa la sogs pa'i rim pa bkod pa las/_'od zer spros pas 'gro ba sems can thams cad la phog pas sems can thams cad kyi las dang nyon mongs pa thams cad sbyangs/_khyad par du yang sems can tshe zad pa thams cad tshe bsrings te/_nang sems kyi dkyil 'khor du bcug nas dbang bskur bas

[\[91b.5\]]sgom mo/_/de'i steng oM b+h+rU~M swA hA/_de'i phyi rim na tshur oM puN+ye puN+ye ma hA puN+ye/_a pa ri mi ta A yu puN+ye/_dz+nyA na saM b+hA ro pa tsi te swA hA zhes pa'i sngags kyi yi ge g.yas nas phreng ba'i tshul du bkod la/_oM tiSh+Tha badzra zhes brjod pas/_de las 'od zer 'phro ba dang 'du ba'i tshul gyis ye shes kyi rdo rje rtse lnga pa cig tu gyur to/_/

[\[92a.1\]]pa lta bu rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa phyag g.yas pas chos 'chad pa'i tshul la g.yon skyabs sbyin mdzad pa'o/_/yang de'i thugs kar zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la sngags kyi yi ge rnams phreng ba'i tshul du bkod pa las/_rdo rje rtse lnga pa yi ge hU~M gis bskyed pa 'phro ba dang

[\[95b.6\]]dkar po cig bri/_thab kyi khor yug gi mu ran la kha khyer sor bzhi pa dang brgyad pa dang bcu drug pa bya'o/_/de la rdo rje'i ra ba rdo rje rtse lnga pa dkar po bri'o/_/'khor lo'i dbus su yang rdo rje dkar po rtse lnga pa bri'o/_/'khor lo'i rtsibs la shar la sogs pa'i rim par rdo rje dang /_

[\[96a\]]
[\[96a.1\]]rgya rnams ni phyi rol bri/_/zhes pa ni phyi rol lha'i snam bu rim pa gnyis par byas pa la phyogs skyong ba rnams dang /_lha chen po brgyad la sogs pa bri/_de'i phyi rim gyi snam bu la gza' brgyad dang /_rgyu skar nyi shu rtsa brgyad ming ngam thig ler 
[\[96a.2\]]bri'o/_/de nas sgo dang rta babs la sogs pa bri'o/_/da ni mchod pa'i khyad par ston te/_bya dang bum pa gang ba dang /_zhes pa la sogs pa gsungs te/_sdig pa bkru ba'i bum pa khro bo rnam par rgyal ba'i bum pa dang /_dbang bskur ba'i bum pa dang /_gzhan yang 
[\[96a.3\]]mchod pa rnam pa lnga dang /_gtor ma mang po dang /_sems can shi ba'i ming gdags pa'i gzhi ste yungs kar dang /_'bras chen dang /_mngar gsum dang /_mar dang /_'o ma dang /_lag pan dang /_rnam gcig tu sha dang /_rus pa dang /_rkang la sogs pa dang /_
[\[96a.4\]]yam shing dang /_dUr ba dang /_ku sha la sogs pa tsan+dan dkar pos kha dog bsgyur la sbyin sreg bya'o/_/da ni chas gos bstan te/_dkar po'i gos ni bgos nas kyang /_/zhes pas ni chas gos rnam pa lnga dkar por bya ba'o/_/sangs rgyas gzugs can mi 'jigs pas/_/
[\[96a.5\]]zhes pa ni rnal 'byor pa rnam par snang mdzad dam/_/bcom ldan 'das rdo rje cher bsgoms la sbyin sreg bya'o/_/ci'i phyir snyam pa la/_ngan song rgyud na gnas pa yi/_/zhes pa la sogs pa'o/_/cis bya ba ni mar dang /_'o mas zhes pa la sogs pas so/_/
[\[96a.6\]]ci tsam du snyam pa la/_de ni bde 'gror zhes pa'i bar du'o/_/de ni zhi ba'i las so/_/da ni rgyas pa'i las bya ste/_tshe 'das pa ngan song las thar nas de'i bsod nams dang /_ye shes spel pa'i phyir rgyas pa'i las bya'o/_/mkhas pas rgyas pa 
[\[96a.7\]]bya dgos te/_/zhes pas mkhas pa ni sgrub pa po las dang bya ba la mkhas pa'o/_/rgyas pa ni dpal dang 'byor pa 'phel bar ro/_/ji ltar 'phel bar bya ba bstan pa'i phyir/_khru do'am ni khru bzhi pa/_/de ltar mchog ni khru brgyad par/_byas nas zhes pa ni me 
[\[96b\]]
[\[96b.1\]]thab kyi tshad do/_/dkyil 'khor gyi phyir tshad ni rab la khru sum cu rtsa gnyis pa'o/_/gzhan gyis ni de las brtsams nas shes par bya'o/_/dbyibs ji ltar snyam pa la/_hom khung gru bzhi pa/_kun du mu ran ldan par bya/_/zhes pa ni mu ran ni kha khyer te/_tha ma la sor brgyad phyir bskyed/_sor brgyad 
[\[96b.2\]]kha khyer du lhag par brtsig_/sor brgyad phyi'i kha khyer la'o/_/de'i phyi rol gyi dkyil 'khor la lha'i snam bu dang /_sgo dang /_rta babs dang bcas par bri bar bya'o/_/'bring dang rab kyis nyis 'gyur du bskyed par bya'o/_/de dbus pad+ma rin chen can/_/'od zer ser po ldan par bri/_/
[\[96b.3\]]zhes pa ni nang du pad+ma'i steng du rin po che kha dog ser por bri bar bya'o/_/nang gi kha khyer la rin po che/_steng gi kha khyer la pad+ma bri bar bya'o/_/phyi rol du ni rigs lnga so so'i phyag mtshan bri bar bya zhes pa ni dbus kyi rin po che'i phyi rol pad+ma'i steng du rigs lnga'i phyag rgya 'khor lo/_
[\[96b.4\]]rdo rje/_rin po che/_pad+ma/_rgya gram rnams bri bar bya'o/_/de ltar phyi yi lha rnams dang /_/lcags kyu la sogs bri bar bya/_/zhes pa ni gong gi zhi ba ltar shes par bya'o/_/gos rnams ser por bsgyur nas kyang /_/zhes pa la sogs pa go sla'o/_/rgyas pa'i las so/_/
[\[96b.5\]]da ni mtho ris thob pa de'i dbang du thams cad bsdu zhing dga' bar bya ba'i phyir dbang gi las bstan te/_hom khung gzhu yi dbyibs 'dra la/_/khru gang ba'am khru do pa/_/khru bzhir byas pa de yi dbus/_/zhes pa la sogs pa la/_hom khung zla ba kham pa lta bur bya ste/_chung ba 
[\[96b.6\]]ni khru gang ba'o/_/'bring ni khru do pa'o/_/rab ni khru bzhi pa'o/_/de'i dbus su pad+ma dmar po de'i dbus su mda' dang bcas pa'i gzhu bri'o/_/khor yug la ni mda' dang bcas pa'i gzhu kha dog dmar po bri bar bya'o/_/sngags rdzogs pa ni brtan par yang /_/phyi rol de 
[\[96b.7\]]dang 'dra bar bya/_/zhes pa ni phyi rol la sogs pa dang /_lha dgod pa ni zhi ba ltar bri bar bya'o/_/dmar por bsgyur ba'i gos bgos la/_/zhes pa ni chas gos dmar por bya'o/_/me tog dmar po'am pad+ma zhes pa la sogs pa ni rdzas te go sla'o/_/dbang gi sbyin sreg bya'o//_//
[\[97a\]]
[\[97a.1\]]da ni tshar gcod pa'i drag po'i las bstan te/_de la gdon rnams gzhom pa'i phyir/_drag po'i las ni brtsam par bya/_/zhes pa ni bar chad kyi bgegs tshar bcad pa'i phyir zhes pa'o/_/phyed dam gsum mam khru gsum mam/_/de ltar dam pa khru dgu bya/_/zhes pa ni rab 'bring gi tshad 
[\[97a.2\]]do/_zur gsum dang ni ldan pa la/_/zhes pa ni dbyibs te gru gsum du bya'o/_/dbus su rdo rje rtse dgu pa/_/rtse gsum pa dang rgya gram pa'i/_/zhes pa la sogs pa la/_dbus su rdo rje rtse dgu pa de'i phyogs bzhir rdo rje rtse gsum pa dang /_rdo rje rgya gram dang /_rdo rje dbyug pa dang /_rtse gsum pa ste/_tri shU la'i mgul du mi 
[\[97a.3\]]mgos mtshan pa rdo rje dang ldan par byas pa'o/_/mu khyud la ni dgra sta dang /_rdo rje rtse gcig pas bskor ba'o/_/gzhan yang zhi ba ltar thams cad bri bar bya'o/_/rdzas ni go sla'o/_/gos nag bgos shing khros nas kyang /_/zhes pa ni chas gos bstan pa'o/_/'jig rten gsum las rnam rgyal bsgyur ba zhes 
[\[97a.4\]]pa ni ting nge 'dzin bstan pa'o/_/de nas de yi sdig bcom lus/_/zhes pa la sogs pa ni go sla'o/_/drag po'i sbyin sreg go_/mchod pa dang ting nge 'dzin gyi rim pa ni 'og nas 'byung ba nyi ma me'i las shes par bya'o/_/de nas tshangs pa la sogs pa/_/zhes pa nas/_smos kyang ci 'tshal zhes pa'i bar gyis ni 
[\[97a.5\]]'khor bsrung bar dam bcas pa ston/_gang gis brtag pa'i rgyal po zhes pa nas/_de bzhin gshegs pa lags par 'tshal bar bgyi'o zhes pa ni rgyud kyi don 'di gang na spyod par bkur bar bstan par bgyi'o zhes pa'o/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das zhes pa nas/_legs par sgrubs shig 
[\[97a.6\]]ces pa'i bar gyis ni bcom ldan 'das kyis gtad nas bsgrub par bstan to/_/de ni rgyud kyi phan yon bstan pa'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas ngan song thams cad yongs su sbyong ba'i gzi brjid kyi rgyal po sku gsum gyi gtsug tor gyi rgyud las rgyud phyi 
[\[97a.7\]]ma'i bshad pa gsal ba'i sgron ma zhes bya ba slob dpon kun dga' snying pos mdzad pa rdzogs so//_//rgya gar gyi mkhan po ku mA ra ka la sha dang /_bod kyi lo tsA ba khyung po chos brtson gyis bsgyur cing zhus te gtan la phab pa'o//_//
